namespace = british_dictates

british_dictates.1 = { # Expand plantations
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = british_dictates.1.t
	desc = british_dictates.1.d
	flavor = british_dictates.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "asia_farmers_market"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		c:BIC ?= this
		is_subject = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = no_overlord_dictate_tt
			NOR = {
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_plantations
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_military
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_law
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_universities
			}
		}
		has_government_type = gov_chartered_company
		NOT = {
			has_variable = british_dictate_cooldown
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				top_overlord.market_capital.market = {
					mg:opium = { market_goods_pricier > 0 }
				}
				country_has_building_type_levels = {
					target = bt:building_opium_plantation
					value >= 10
				}
				any_scope_state = {
					can_construct_building = building_opium_plantation
				}
				ROOT.free_opium_producing_arable_land >= 50
			}
			AND = {
				top_overlord.market_capital.market = {
					mg:tea = { market_goods_pricier > 0 }
				}
				country_has_building_type_levels = {
					target = bt:building_tea_plantation
					value >= 10
				}
				any_scope_state = {
					can_construct_building = building_tea_plantation
				}
				ROOT.free_tea_producing_arable_land >= 50
			}
			AND = {
				top_overlord.market_capital.market = {
					mg:dye = { market_goods_pricier > 0 }
				}
				country_has_building_type_levels = {
					target = bt:building_dye_plantation
					value >= 10
				}
				any_scope_state = {
					can_construct_building = building_dye_plantation
				}
				ROOT.free_dye_producing_arable_land >= 50
			}
			AND = {
				top_overlord.market_capital.market = {
					mg:fabric = { market_goods_pricier > 0 }
				}
				country_has_building_type_levels = {
					target = bt:building_cotton_plantation
					value >= 10
				}
				any_scope_state = {
					can_construct_building = building_cotton_plantation
				}
				ROOT.free_cotton_producing_arable_land >= 50
			}
			AND = {
				top_overlord.market_capital.market = {
					mg:tobacco = { market_goods_pricier > 0 }
				}
				country_has_building_type_levels = {
					target = bt:building_tobacco_plantation
					value >= 10
				}
				any_scope_state = {
					can_construct_building = building_tobacco_plantation
				}
				ROOT.free_tobacco_producing_arable_land >= 50
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			is_subject_of = scope:overlord_scope
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { name = british_dictate_cooldown days = long_modifier_time }
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = overlord_scope
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_in_government = yes
				}
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = board_of_control_character
				}
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					top_overlord.market_capital.market = {
						mg:opium = { market_goods_pricier > -0.25 }
					}
					country_has_building_group_levels = {
						target = bt:building_opium_plantation
						value >= 10
					}
					ROOT.free_opium_producing_arable_land >= 50
				}
				set_variable = { name = dictate_plantation_type value = 1 }
			}
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					top_overlord.market_capital.market = {
						mg:tea = { market_goods_pricier > -0.25 }
					}
					country_has_building_type_levels = {
						target = bt:building_tea_plantation
						value >= 10
					}
					ROOT.free_tea_producing_arable_land >= 50
				}
				set_variable = { name = dictate_plantation_type value = 2 }
			}
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					top_overlord.market_capital.market = {
						mg:dye = { market_goods_pricier > -0.25 }
					}
					country_has_building_type_levels = {
						target = bt:building_dye_plantation
						value >= 10
					}
					ROOT.free_dye_producing_arable_land >= 50
				}
				set_variable = { name = dictate_plantation_type value = 3 }
			}
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					top_overlord.market_capital.market = {
						mg:fabric = { market_goods_pricier > -0.25 }
					}
					country_has_building_type_levels = {
						target = bt:building_cotton_plantation
						value >= 10
					}
					ROOT.free_cotton_producing_arable_land >= 50
				}
				set_variable = { name = dictate_plantation_type value = 4 }
			}
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					top_overlord.market_capital.market = {
						mg:tobacco = { market_goods_pricier > -0.25 }
					}
					country_has_building_type_levels = {
						target = bt:building_tobacco_plantation
						value >= 10
					}
					ROOT.free_tobacco_producing_arable_land >= 50
				}
				set_variable = { name = dictate_plantation_type value = 5 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = british_dictates.1.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 8
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = adds_british_dictate_plantations_je_tt
			add_journal_entry = { type = je_british_dictate_plantations }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = british_dictates.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
			}
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = -5
				factor = appeasement_british_dictate_rejected
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = root.top_overlord value = -20
		}
	}
}

british_dictates.2 = { # Develop military
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = british_dictates.2.t
	desc = british_dictates.2.d
	flavor = british_dictates.2.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_redcoats"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		c:BIC ?= this
		is_subject = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = no_overlord_dictate_tt
			NOR = {
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_plantations
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_military
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_law
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_universities
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = british_dictate_cooldown
		}
		top_overlord = {
			is_at_war = yes
			any_enemy_in_diplo_play = {
				army_power_projection >= PREV.army_power_projection
			}
		}
		country_has_building_type_levels = {
			target = bt:building_barrack
			value  <= 300
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			is_subject_of = scope:overlord_scope
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { name = british_dictate_cooldown days = long_modifier_time }
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = overlord_scope
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_in_government = yes
				}
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = board_of_control_character
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = british_dictates.1.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 8
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = adds_british_dictate_military_je_tt
			add_journal_entry = { type = je_british_dictate_military }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = british_dictates.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
			}
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = -5
				factor = appeasement_british_dictate_rejected
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = root.top_overlord value = -20
		}
	}
}

british_dictates.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	title = british_dictates.3.t
	desc = british_dictates.3.d
	flavor = british_dictates.3.f

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { name = british_dictate_cooldown days = long_modifier_time }
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = overlord_scope
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_in_government = yes
				}
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = board_of_control_character
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOR = {
				exists = imposed_law
				exists = imposer_of_law
			}
			NOT = {
				is_subject_of = scope:overlord_scope
			}
		}
	}

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by on_impose_law
	}

	option = { # start enacting immediately
		name = british_dictates.3.a.1
		trigger = {
			enacting_any_law = no
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			always = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = adds_british_dictate_law_je_tt
			add_journal_entry = { type = je_british_dictate_law }
		}
		start_enactment = imposed_law.type
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = agreeable_to_imposition
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = law_imposition_events.1.journal_entry_hint
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_british_dictate_law ?= {
						has_modifier = foreign_legislative_interference
					}
				}
				je:je_british_dictate_law ?= {
					remove_modifier = foreign_legislative_interference
					add_modifier = foreign_legislative_interference_enactment
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.10 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.15 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.20 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.25 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.30 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.35 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.40 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # cancel current law and start enacting immediately
		name = british_dictates.3.a.2
		trigger = {
			enacting_any_law = yes
		}
		cancel_enactment = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = adds_british_dictate_law_je_tt
			add_journal_entry = { type = je_british_dictate_law }
		}
		start_enactment = imposed_law.type
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = agreeable_to_imposition
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = law_imposition_events.1.journal_entry_hint
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_british_dictate_law ?= {
						has_modifier = foreign_legislative_interference
					}
				}
				je:je_british_dictate_law ?= {
					remove_modifier = foreign_legislative_interference
					add_modifier = foreign_legislative_interference_enactment
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.10 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.15 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.20 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.25 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.30 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.35 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.40 < "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.10 < enactment_chance
				}
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.15 < enactment_chance
				}
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.20 < enactment_chance
				}
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.25 < enactment_chance
				}
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.30 < enactment_chance
				}
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.35 < enactment_chance
				}
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.40 < enactment_chance
				}
				add = -10
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # defer
		name = british_dictates.3.b
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = adds_british_dictate_law_je_tt
			add_journal_entry = { type = je_british_dictate_law }
		}
		scope:foreign_imposition_country = {
			post_notification = imposition_deferred
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = law_imposition_events.1.journal_entry_hint
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					enacting_any_law = no
				}
				add = -5
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # refuse
		name = british_dictates.3.c
		change_relations = {
			country = imposer_of_law
			value = -30
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = imposer_of_law }
			add_liberty_desire = 5
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { power_bloc ?= imposer_of_law.power_bloc }
			power_bloc = {
				add_cohesion_percent = -0.05
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = country_imposed_upon
		imposer_of_law = {
			trigger_event = { id = law_imposition_events.3 popup = yes  }
		}
		imposed_law = {
			cancel_imposition = yes
		}
		every_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				target = scope:foreign_imposition_country
				OR = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
				}
			}
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = -5
				factor = appeasement_british_dictate_rejected
			}
		}
		every_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				target = scope:foreign_imposition_country
				OR = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
				}
			}
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = 3
				factor = appeasement_british_dictate_rejected
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.1 > "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
					liberty_desire > 40
				}
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.1 > "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
					liberty_desire > 60
				}
				add = 50
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.1 > "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
					scope:foreign_imposition_country = { relations:root <= relations_threshold:neutral }
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.1 > "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
					scope:foreign_imposition_country = { relations:root <= relations_threshold:poor }
				}
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					0.1 > "enactment_chance_for_law(imposed_law.type)"
					scope:foreign_imposition_country = { relations:root <= relations_threshold:cold }
				}
				add = 50
			}
		}
	}
}

british_dictates.4 = { # Develop universities
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = british_dictates.4.t
	desc = british_dictates.4.d
	flavor = british_dictates.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_rich_and_poor"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		c:BIC ?= this
		is_subject = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = no_overlord_dictate_tt
			NOR = {
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_plantations
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_military
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_law
				has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_universities
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = british_dictate_cooldown
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_university
					level >= 3
				}
			}
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_university
					level >= 3
				}
			}
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_MADRAS
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_university
					level >= 3
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			is_subject_of = scope:overlord_scope
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { name = british_dictate_cooldown days = long_modifier_time }
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = overlord_scope
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_in_government = yes
				}
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = board_of_control_character
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = british_dictates.1.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 8
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = adds_british_dictate_universities_je_tt
			add_journal_entry = { type = je_british_dictate_universities }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = british_dictates.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
			}
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = -5
				factor = appeasement_british_dictate_rejected
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = root.top_overlord value = -20
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = caste_conflict

# je_dravidian_movement starting event
caste_conflict.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3


	title = caste_conflict.1.t
	desc = caste_conflict.1.d
	flavor = caste_conflict.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_intercommunal_tensions"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by JE

	}

	immediate = {
        ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
            save_scope_as = rural_folk_ig_scope
        }
        top_overlord = {
        	save_scope_as = top_overlord_scope
        }
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
        OR = {
        	NOT = { has_social_hierarchy = british_indian_caste_system }
        	is_subject = no
        }
	}

	option = {
		name = caste_conflict.1.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = rural_folk_pol_str_gain
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				game_date >= 1904.1.1
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = erode_venkatappa_ramasamy_appears_tt
				create_character = {
					template = BIC_erode_venkatappa_ramasamy # E. V. Ramasamy aka Periyar
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				game_date <= 1904.1.1
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = an_agitator_appears_tt
				create_character = {
					interest_group = ig_rural_folk
	    			ideology = ideology_social_democrat
	    			is_agitator = yes
	    			culture = cu:tamil
    				religion = rel:atheist
				    traits = {
				        direct
				        brave
				        erudite
				    }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc_feature = lobbies
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
				}
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = { is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord }
				change_appeasement = {
					amount = -1
					factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caste_conflict.1.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = current_system_supported
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				game_date >= 1904.1.1
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = erode_venkatappa_ramasamy_appears_tt
				create_character = {
					template = BIC_erode_venkatappa_ramasamy # E. V. Ramasamy aka Periyar
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				game_date <= 1904.1.1
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = an_agitator_appears_tt
				create_character = {
					interest_group = ig_rural_folk
	    			ideology = ideology_social_democrat
	    			is_agitator = yes
	    			culture = cu:tamil
    				religion = rel:atheist
    				traits = {
				        direct
				        brave
				        erudite
				    }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				top_overlord = {
					any_subject_or_below = {
						OR = {
							c:BIC ?= this
							c:BHT ?= this
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = liberty_desire_hit
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# The Non-Brahmin Manifesto
caste_conflict.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:revelvant_state

	duration = 3

	title = caste_conflict.4.t
	desc = caste_conflict.4.d
	flavor = caste_conflict.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = revelvant_state
		}
		top_overlord ={
			save_scope_as = top_overlord_scope
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = rural_folk_leader_scope
			}
            save_scope_as = rural_folk_ig_scope
        }
        if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = GBR_annie_besant
					}
				}
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = GBR_annie_besant
					}
					save_scope_as = intelligentsia_leader_scope
				}
				save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig_scope
			}
		}
		else = {
			ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = intelligentsia_leader_scope
				}
	            save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig_scope
	        }
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		NOT = { has_social_hierarchy = british_indian_caste_system }
	}

	option = {
		name = caste_conflict.4.a
		default_option = yes
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:top_overlord_scope
			value = 10
		}
		scope:intelligentsia_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = argument_to_be_made
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caste_conflict.4.b
		scope:revelvant_state ={
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				strata = middle
			}
		}
		scope:rural_folk_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = argument_to_be_made
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# Untouchables and Outcasts
caste_conflict.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = caste_conflict.5.t
	desc = caste_conflict.5.d
	flavor = caste_conflict.5.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_intercommunal_tensions"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				game_date >= 1917.1.1
			}
			create_character = {
				template = BIC_bhimrao_ramji_ambedkar # B. R. Ambedkar
				on_created = {
					set_variable = satyagraha_var
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				interest_group = ig_rural_folk
    			ideology = ideology_social_democrat
    			is_agitator = yes
    			culture = cu:marathi
    			religion = rel:hindu
			    traits = {
			        direct
			        literary
			        expert_political_operator
			    }
				on_created = {
					set_variable = satyagraha_var
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = satyagraha_var
			}
			save_scope_as = character_scope
		}

	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { has_social_hierarchy = british_indian_caste_system }
	}

	option = {
		name = caste_conflict.5.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:character_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = opposition_to_untouchability
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			pop_type = clergymen
			value = large_radicals
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caste_conflict.5.b
		scope:character_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = opposition_to_untouchability
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = priestly_polluted_water
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

communal_divides_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = communal_divides_events.2.t
	desc = communal_divides_events.2.d
	flavor = communal_divides_events.2.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_intercommunal_tensions"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	trigger = {
		# triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
					OR = {
						country_has_state_religion = rel:sunni
						country_has_state_religion = rel:shiite
					}
					NOT = {
						is_subject_of = root
					}
				}
			}
			ordered_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
					OR = {
						country_has_state_religion = rel:sunni
						country_has_state_religion = rel:shiite
					}
					NOT = {
						is_subject_of = root
					}
				}
				order_by = gdp
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
				save_scope_as = muslim_indian_country_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = communal_divides_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country_in_india = {
					OR = {
						country_has_state_religion = rel:sunni
						country_has_state_religion = rel:shiite
					}
					NOT = {
						is_subject_of = root
					}
				}
			}
			every_country_in_india = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						country_has_state_religion = rel:sunni
						country_has_state_religion = rel:shiite
					}
					NOT = {
						is_subject_of = root
					}
				}
				change_relations = {
					country = root
					value = -30
				}
				create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
					type = catalyst_event_negative
					target = root
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_intercommunal_violence
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = communal_divides_events.2.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_intercommunal_violence
			days = long_modifier_time
			multiplier = 1.5
		}
	}
}

communal_divides_events.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:relevant_state

	duration = 3

	title = communal_divides_events.3.t
	desc = communal_divides_events.3.d
	flavor = communal_divides_events.3.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_intercommunal_tensions"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	trigger = {
		any_political_movement = {
		    is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
		}
		any_political_movement = {
		    is_political_movement_type = movement_muslim_nationalist
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			any_scope_pop = {
				religion = rel:sunni
			}
			any_scope_pop = {
				religion = rel:hindu
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					religion = rel:sunni
				}
				any_scope_pop = {
					religion = rel:hindu
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
			}
			save_scope_as = india_congress_scope
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_muslim_nationalist
			}
			save_scope_as = muslim_league_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = communal_divides_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = rel:sunni
				value = medium_radicals
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = rel:hindu
				value = medium_radicals
			}
			random_list = {
				1 = {
					add_modifier = {
						name = mob_violence_modifier
						days = short_modifier_time
					}
				}
				1 = {
				}
			}
		}
		scope:india_congress_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = exploiting_sectarian_divisions
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:muslim_league_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = exploiting_sectarian_divisions
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = communal_divides_events.3.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = rel:sunni
				value = small_radicals
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = rel:hindu
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
		scope:india_congress_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = sectarian_infighting_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:muslim_league_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = inc_congress_influx
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = mob_violence_modifier
				days = short_modifier_time
				multiplier = 1.5
			}

				religion = rel:sunni
				value = small_radicals
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = rel:hindu
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
		scope:muslim_league_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = outraged_over_power_sharing
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = communal_divides_events.5.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 33
		}
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = mob_violence_modifier
				days = short_modifier_time

				value = small_radicals
			}

				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	}

			any_scope_pop = {

			}
		}

		    is_political_movement_type = movement_muslim_nationalist
		}

		    is_political_movement_type = movement_hindu_nationalist
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					religion = rel:sunni
				}
				any_scope_pop = {
					religion = rel:hindu

		random_political_movement = {

				name = losing_grip_over_urban_administrations
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {

			base = 33
		}
		scope:relevant_state = {

				value = small_radicals
			}

﻿namespace = federation_of_india

federation_of_india.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	duration = 3

	title = federation_of_india.1.t
	desc = federation_of_india.1.d
	flavor = federation_of_india.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_urban_scene"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		overlord = {
			save_scope_as = india_scope
		}
		save_scope_as = princely_state_scope
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:india_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = federation_of_india.1.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					scope:india_scope = {
						is_player = yes
					}
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					liberty_desire < define:NDiplomacy|WAR_GOAL_INFAMY_LOW_LIBERTY_DESIRE_THRESHOLD
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					liberty_desire < 15
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					liberty_desire < 5
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					liberty_desire = 0
				}
				add = 50
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					"relations(scope:india_scope)" >= relations_threshold:amicable
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					"relations(scope:india_scope)" >= relations_threshold:friendly
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						ruler = { age >= define:NCharacters|OLD_AGE }
						ruler = { age < define:NCharacters|ADULT_AGE }
					}
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					ruler = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = psychological_affliction
							has_trait = cancer
							has_trait = senile
							has_trait = wounded
						}
					}
				}
				add = 25
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_player = yes
			}
			play_as = scope:india_scope
		}
		scope:india_scope = {
			post_notification = joins_union_of_india
			change_variable = { name = indian_states_annexed add = 1 }
			set_variable = update_privy_purse
			annex = ROOT
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = federation_of_india.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
		}
		add_liberty_desire = 10
		scope:india_scope = {
			change_relations = {
				country = ROOT
				value = -25
			}
		}
		scope:india_scope = {
			post_notification = refuses_to_join_union_of_india
		}
	}
}

federation_of_india.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	duration = 3

	title = federation_of_india.3.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = federation_of_india.3.d_cr
				trigger = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = federation_of_india.3.d_infamy
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = federation_of_india.3.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_urban_scene"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = 365 }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = india_scope
		ordered_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
			limit = {
				is_princely_state = yes
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
			}
			order_by = country_rank
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = relevant_country
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = federation_of_india.3.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = add_claims_on_india_tt
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
					NOT = {
						owner = ROOT
						has_claim_by = ROOT
					}
				}
				state_region = {
					add_claim = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = upset_every_princely_state_federation_tt
			every_princely_state = {
				change_relations = {
					country = ROOT
					value = -10
				}
				add_liberty_desire = 25
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:relevant_country
			}
			create_incident = {
				value = 15
				country = scope:relevant_country
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = federation_of_india.3.b
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = add_claims_on_india_tt
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
					NOT = {
						owner = ROOT
						has_claim_by = ROOT
					}
				}
				state_region = {
					add_claim = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = upset_every_princely_state_federation_tt
			every_princely_state = {
				change_relations = {
					country = ROOT
					value = -10
				}
				add_liberty_desire = 25
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:relevant_country
			}
			create_incident = {
				value = 15
				country = scope:relevant_country
			}
		}
	}
}

				liberty_desire < define:NDiplomacy|WAR_GOAL_INFAMY_LOW_LIBERTY_DESIRE_THRESHOLD

			base = 1
		}

		name = federation_of_india.4.b

			base = 0
		}

			make_independent = yes
		}
	}

﻿namespace = indian_famines

indian_famines.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = indian_famines.1.t
	desc = indian_famines.1.d
	flavor = indian_famines.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "asia_dead_cattle_poor_harvest"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			c:BIC ?= this
			any_primary_culture = {
				has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
			}
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			OR = {
				region = sr:region_madras
				region = sr:region_bombay
				region = sr:region_bengal
				region = sr:region_central_india
				region = sr:region_punjab
			}
			has_famine = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_madras
					region = sr:region_bombay
					region = sr:region_bengal
					region = sr:region_central_india
					region = sr:region_punjab
				}
				has_famine = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = indian_famine_state_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = indian_famines.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:indian_famine_state_scope = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = indian_famines.1.b
		scope:indian_famine_state_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = reduced_taxation_due_to_famine
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = indian_famines.1.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = not_recently_had_war
				is_at_war = no
				NOT = {
					has_variable = recently_had_war
				}
			}
		}
		scope:indian_famine_state_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = no_taxation_due_to_famine
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = famine_relief_aid_costs
			days = normal_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_very_small
		}
	}
}

indian_famines.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = indian_famines.2.t
	desc = indian_famines.2.d
	flavor = indian_famines.2.f

	event_image = {
		video = "asia_dead_cattle_poor_harvest"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = hunger_strike_var
		}
		c:BIC ?= this
		any_scope_state = {
			OR = {
				region = sr:region_madras
				region = sr:region_bombay
				region = sr:region_bengal
				region = sr:region_central_india
				region = sr:region_punjab
			}
			has_famine = yes
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_building_type = building_cotton_plantation
					is_building_type = building_dye_plantation
				}
				level >= 5
			}
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			culture = {
				has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
			}
			has_role = agitator
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = hunger_strike_var
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_madras
					region = sr:region_bombay
					region = sr:region_bengal
					region = sr:region_central_india
					region = sr:region_punjab
				}
				has_famine = yes
				any_scope_building = {
					OR = {
						is_building_type = building_cotton_plantation
						is_building_type = building_dye_plantation
					}
					level >= 5
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = indian_famine_state_scope
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				culture = {
					has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
				}
				has_role = agitator
			}
			save_scope_as = hunger_strike_agitator_scope
			set_variable = hunger_striker_var
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = indian_famines.2.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = indian_famines.2.tt
		show_as_tooltip = {
			ordered_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_madras
						region = sr:region_bombay
						region = sr:region_bengal
						region = sr:region_central_india
						region = sr:region_punjab
					}
					has_famine = yes
					any_scope_building = {
						OR = {
							is_building_type = building_cotton_plantation
							is_building_type = building_dye_plantation
						}
						level >= 5
					}
				}
				order_by = gdp
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
				add_modifier = {
					name = temporary_switch_to_food_crops
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
				add_loyalists_in_state = {
					value = small_radicals
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_madras
						region = sr:region_bombay
						region = sr:region_bengal
						region = sr:region_central_india
						region = sr:region_punjab
					}
					has_famine = yes
					any_scope_building = {
						OR = {
							is_building_type = building_cotton_plantation
							is_building_type = building_dye_plantation
						}
						level >= 5
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = temporary_switch_to_food_crops
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
				add_loyalists_in_state = {
					value = small_radicals
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = bowed_to_food_crop_demands
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = indian_famines.2.b
		random_list = {
			9 = {
				trigger_event = { id = indian_famines.3 popup = yes days = 30 }
			}
			1 = {
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = following_applied_to_every_owned_state
		show_as_tooltip = {
			ordered_scope_state = {
				add_radicals_in_state = {
					value = small_radicals
				}
				order_by = gdp
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_scope_state = {
				add_radicals_in_state = {
					value = small_radicals
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
				}
				has_role = agitator
				has_variable = hunger_striker_var
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = indian_famines.3.a
		default_option = yes
		random_list = {
			9 = {
				add_radicals = {
					value = small_radicals
				}
			}
			1 = {
				scope:hunger_strike_agitator_scope = {
					kill_character = yes
				}
				add_radicals = {
					value = medium_radicals
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = indian_famines.3.b
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = bowed_to_hunger_strike
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = food_crop_system_modifier
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

				name = tax_breaks_for_warehouses
				days = normal_modifier_time

				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

﻿namespace = india_home_rule_events

india_home_rule_events.1 = { # The Indian Home Rule Act
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	duration = 3

	title = india_home_rule_events.1.t
	desc = india_home_rule_events.1.d
	flavor = india_home_rule_events.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_urban_scene"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		change_tag = BHT
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = south_asian_primary_cultures_tt
			every_scope_culture = {
				limit = {
					has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
					save_temporary_scope_as = temp_culture_scope
					ROOT = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = {
								is_homeland = scope:temp_culture_scope
							}
						}
					}
				}
				ROOT = {
					add_primary_culture = prev
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_state_atheism
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_percent_country = {
						target = rel:hindu
						value >= 0.5
					}
				}
				set_state_religion = rel:hindu
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					religion_percent_country = {
						target = rel:sunni
						value >= 0.5
					}
				}
				set_state_religion = rel:sunni
			}
			else = {
				set_state_religion = rel:hindu
			}
		}
		india_independence_ig_effects = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = modifier_india_crown_rule
			}
			remove_modifier = modifier_india_crown_rule
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = modifier_india_company_rule
			}
			remove_modifier = modifier_india_company_rule
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = modifier_india_company_rule_upgraded
			}
			remove_modifier = modifier_india_company_rule_upgraded
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = modifier_indian_imperial_police
			}
			remove_modifier = modifier_indian_imperial_police
		}
		recalculate_pop_ig_support = yes
		call_election = {
			months = 6
		}
	}
}

	trigger = {

	}

	cancellation_trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		c:BIC ?= {
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
				}

			}
		}
		save_scope_as = india_overlord_scope
		je:je_india_nationalism_britain ?= {
			save_scope_as = journal_entry_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					scope:journal_entry_scope ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 50
					}
				}
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					scope:journal_entry_scope ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 60
					}
				}
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					scope:journal_entry_scope ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 70
					}
				}
				add = -25
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					scope:journal_entry_scope ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 80
					}
				}
				add = -25
			}
			modifier = { # AI will always accept when above 90
				trigger = {
					scope:journal_entry_scope ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 90
					}
				}
				add = -100
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = india_bar_retreat_25_tt
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			custom_tooltip = due_to_current_india_bar_progress_tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:journal_entry_scope ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 50
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" < 70
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope = {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = -1
						factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
					}
				}
				add_small_indian_radicals = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:journal_entry_scope ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 70
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" < 90
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope = {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = -3
						factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
					}
				}
				add_medium_indian_radicals = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:journal_entry_scope ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 90
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope = {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = -5
						factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
					}
				}
				add_large_indian_radicals = yes
			}
			else = {
				scope:inc_scope = {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = -1
						factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
					}
				}
				add_small_indian_radicals = yes
			}
		}
		je:je_india_nationalism_britain ?= {
			add_progress = { value = -25 name = je_india_home_rule_progressbar }
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			post_notification = overlord_government_reform_denied
			je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
				add_progress = { value = -25 name = je_india_home_rule_progressbar }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {

india_home_rule_events.3 = { # Jallianwala Bagh Massacre
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:jallianwala_bagh_state

	duration = 3

	title = india_home_rule_events.3.t
	desc = india_home_rule_events.3.d
	flavor = india_home_rule_events.3.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_redcoats"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	# Triggered by JE

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_HILL_PUNJAB
				}
			}
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_HILL_PUNJAB
				}
				save_scope_as = jallianwala_bagh_state
				random_scope_culture = {
					limit = {
						has_homeland = prev
					}
					save_scope_as = relevant_culture
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
				}
				save_scope_as = jallianwala_bagh_state
				random_scope_culture = {
					limit = {
						has_homeland = prev
					}
					save_scope_as = relevant_culture
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				game_date > 1900.1.1
				game_date < 1924.1.1
			}
			create_character = {
				template = BIC_reginald_dyer
				on_created = {
					set_character_busy_and_immortal = yes
					place_character_in_void = 2400
					set_variable = dyer_variable
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				is_general = yes
				culture = cu:british
				interest_group = ig_armed_forces
				ideology = ideology_authoritarian
				traits = {
					war_criminal
					cruel
					plains_commander
				}
				on_created = {
					set_character_busy_and_immortal = yes
					place_character_in_void = 2400
					set_variable = dyer_variable
				}
			}
		}
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = dyer_variable
			}
			save_scope_as = dyer_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = home_rule_is_over_tt
		scope:jallianwala_bagh_state = {
			kill_population_in_state = {
				value = 500
				culture = scope:relevant_culture
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = large_radicals
				culture = scope:relevant_culture
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_constitutionalist
				}
			}
			ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
				remove_ideology = ideology_constitutionalist
				add_ideology = ideology_republican
			}
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_india_massacred_protesters
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = india_home_rule_events.4 days = 2 }
	}
}

	duration = 3

	title = india_home_rule_events.7.t
	desc = india_home_rule_events.7.d
	flavor = india_home_rule_events.7.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_urban_scene"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	# Triggered by event

	immediate = {
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig
		}
		ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= {
			save_scope_as = petty_bourgeoisie_ig
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_political_lobby = {
						is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
					}
				}
			}
			create_political_lobby = {
				type = lobby_anti_overlord
				target = top_overlord
				add_interest_group = ig:ig_intelligentsia
				add_interest_group = ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie
				lobby_formation_reason = diplomacy
			}
			set_variable = { name = inc_formed months = 3 }
		}
		else = {
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							any_lobby_member = {
								is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
							}
						}
					}
					add_lobby_member = ig:ig_intelligentsia
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							any_lobby_member = {
								is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
							}
						}
					}
					add_lobby_member = ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie
				}
			}
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.7.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = inc_formed
			}
			custom_tooltip = independence_lobby_formed_tt
		}
		scope:intelligentsia_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_indian_national_movement_ig
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		scope:petty_bourgeoisie_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_indian_national_movement_ig
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.7.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = inc_formed
			}
			custom_tooltip = independence_lobby_formed_tt
		}
		scope:intelligentsia_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_indian_national_movement_ig
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
		scope:petty_bourgeoisie_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_indian_national_movement_ig
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = 1
				factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
			}
		}
	}
}

	placement = root

	duration = 3

			}

	}
