india_home_rule_events.9 = { # Montagu-Chelmsford Reforms
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = india_home_rule_events.9.t
	desc = india_home_rule_events.9.d
	flavor = india_home_rule_events.9.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_urban_scene"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	# Triggered by decision

	immediate = {
		top_overlord = {
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_in_government = yes
					OR = {
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_no_colonial_affairs
							value > neutral
						}
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_cultural_exclusion
							value > neutral
						}
					}
				}
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = montagu_scope
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = overlord_scope
		}
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = chelmsford_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.9.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			any_political_movement = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
				political_movement_radicalism <= 0.5
			}
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting
		activate_law = law_type:law_elected_bureaucrats
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = simon_commission_in_ten_years_tt
			trigger_event = { id = india_home_rule_events.10 years = 10 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.9.b
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			any_political_movement = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
				political_movement_radicalism > 0.5
			}
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting
		activate_law = law_type:law_elected_bureaucrats
		add_liberty_desire = 5
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = simon_commission_in_ten_years_tt
			top_overlord = {
				trigger_event = { id = india_home_rule_events.10 years = 10 }
			}
		}
	}
}

india_home_rule_events.10 = { # Simon Commission setup
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = india_home_rule_events.10.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_home_rule_events.10.d_1 # Ahead of time
				trigger = {
					has_variable = simon_commission_timer_var
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_home_rule_events.10.d_2 # On time
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = india_home_rule_events.10.f

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = simon_commission_var
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = india_scope
		}
		set_variable = simon_commission_var
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.10.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = simon_commission_conservative
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = commission_sent_conservative_tt
			scope:india_scope ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = india_home_rule_events.11
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.10.b
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			any_interest_group = {
				is_in_government = yes
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
					leader = {
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = ideology_sympathetic_to_indians_tt
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pacifist
								has_progressive_ideology = yes
								has_socialist_ideology = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = simon_commission_liberal
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = commission_sent_liberal_tt
			scope:india_scope ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = india_home_rule_events.11
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

india_home_rule_events.11 = { # Simon Commission
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = india_home_rule_events.11.t

	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_home_rule_events.11.d_1 # Liberal Commission
				trigger = {
					top_overlord = {
						has_variable = simon_commission_liberal
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_home_rule_events.11.d_2 # Conservative Commission
				trigger = {
					top_overlord = {
						has_variable = simon_commission_conservative
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	flavor = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_home_rule_events.11.f_1 # Liberal Commission
				trigger = {
					top_overlord = {
						has_variable = simon_commission_liberal
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_home_rule_events.11.f_2 # Conservative Commission
				trigger = {
					top_overlord = {
						has_variable = simon_commission_conservative
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_urban_scene"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	# Triggered by decision

	immediate = {
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = overlord_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				top_overlord = {
					has_variable = simon_commission_liberal
				}
			}
			top_overlord = {
				if = { # In case Britain has reformed its government in the past three days or something like that.
					limit = {
						any_interest_group = {
							is_in_government = yes
							OR = {
								is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
								leader = {
									OR = {
										has_progressive_ideology = yes
										has_socialist_ideology = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					random_interest_group = {
						limit = {
							is_in_government = yes
							OR = {
								is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
								leader = {
									OR = {
										has_progressive_ideology = yes
										has_socialist_ideology = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						leader = {
							set_variable = simon_var
							save_scope_as = simon_scope
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					random_interest_group = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
								leader = {
									OR = {
										has_progressive_ideology = yes
										has_socialist_ideology = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						leader = {
							set_variable = simon_var
							save_scope_as = simon_scope
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			top_overlord = {
				random_interest_group = {
					limit = {
						is_in_government = yes
					}
					leader = {
						set_variable = simon_var
						save_scope_as = simon_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
				}
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
				}
				save_scope_as = inc_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.11.a
		trigger = {
			top_overlord = {
				has_variable = simon_commission_conservative
			}
		}
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_india_home_rule
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_simon_commission_embraced
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 75
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = 3
						factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
					}
				}
				add_small_indian_radicals = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 75
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" > 50
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = 1
						factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
					}
				}
				add_small_indian_radicals = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 50
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" > 25
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = -2
						factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
					}
				}
				add_medium_indian_radicals = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 25
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = -4
						factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
					}
				}
				add_large_indian_radicals = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_small_indian_radicals = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					game_date > 1928.1.1
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = india_events.11
					days = 30
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.11.b
		trigger = {
			top_overlord = {
				has_variable = simon_commission_conservative
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_simon_commission_boycott
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_india_home_rule
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 50
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" > 25
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = -2
						factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
					}
				}
				add_medium_indian_radicals = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 25
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" > 10
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = -4
						factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
					}
				}
				add_large_indian_radicals = yes
			}
			else = {
				add_small_indian_radicals = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_medium_indian_radicals = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					game_date > 1928.1.1
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = india_events.11
					days = 30
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.11.c
		trigger = {
			top_overlord = {
				has_variable = simon_commission_liberal
			}
		}
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_india_home_rule
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_simon_commission_embraced
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 75
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = 6
						factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
					}
				}
				add_medium_indian_loyalists = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 75
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" > 50
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = 4
						factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
					}
				}
				add_small_indian_loyalists = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 50
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" > 25
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = 2
						factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 25
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = 1
						factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:inc_scope ?= {
				change_appeasement = {
					amount = 1
					factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
				}
			}
			add_small_indian_loyalists = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.11.e
		trigger = {
			top_overlord = {
				has_variable = simon_commission_liberal
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_india_home_rule
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" >= 75
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = 4
						factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
					}
				}
				add_medium_indian_loyalists = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 75
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" > 50
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = 3
						factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
					}
				}
				add_small_indian_loyalists = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 50
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" > 25
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = 2
						factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 25
					}
				}
				scope:inc_scope ?= {
					change_appeasement = {
						amount = 1
						factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:inc_scope ?= {
				change_appeasement = {
					amount = 1
					factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_home_rule_events.11.g
		trigger = {
			top_overlord = {
				has_variable = simon_commission_liberal
			}
			OR = {
				top_overlord = {
					je:je_india_nationalism_britain = {
						"scripted_bar_progress(je_india_home_rule_progressbar)" <= 15
					}
				}
				# High militancy
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_simon_commission_boycott
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		add_medium_indian_radicals = yes
	}
}
﻿namespace = india_events

# Most of these events use global variables, due to the fact that the BIC tag can disappear if a native-ruled India is created.

india_events.1 = { # Kaisar-i-Hind
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = india_events.1.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_events.1.d_1
				trigger = {
					country_has_voting_franchise = yes
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_events.1.d_2
				trigger = {
					country_has_voting_franchise = no
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_events.1.f_1
				trigger = {
					country_has_voting_franchise = yes
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_events.1.f_2
				trigger = {
					country_has_voting_franchise = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	left_icon = ruler

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		c:GBR ?= this
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
		any_subject_or_below = {
			c:BIC ?= this
			NOT = {
				has_government_type = gov_chartered_company
			}
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_DELHI
			}
		}
		country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_WEST_BENGAL
		OR = {
			py:conservative_party ?= { # Tory Government
				any_member = {
					is_in_government = yes
				}
			}
			country_has_voting_franchise = no
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = empress_of_india
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = ruler_scope
		}
		ordered_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_in_government = yes
			}
			order_by = ig_clout
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = pm_scope
			}
		}
		set_global_variable = empress_of_india
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			scope:ruler_scope = {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:ruler_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = c:BIC
			}
			NOT = {
				c:BIC ?= {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:ruler_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_empress_of_india
					days = very_long_modifier_time
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_emperor_of_india
					days = very_long_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		c:BIC ?= { trigger_event = { id = india_events.2 months = 3 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.1.b
		trigger = {
			country_has_voting_franchise = yes
		}
		scope:pm_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_denied_monarch_title
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			pop_type = aristocrats
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.1.c
		trigger = {
			country_has_voting_franchise = no
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_LOWLANDS # Must have Scotland as well
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_HIGHLANDS
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = empress_or_emperor_of_britain_tt
			set_global_variable = empire_of_great_britain
		}
		scope:ruler_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_empress_of_great_britain
					days = very_long_modifier_time
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_emperor_of_great_britain
					days = very_long_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			interest_group = ig:ig_intelligentsia
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			interest_group = ig:ig_trade_unions
		}
		c:BIC ?= { trigger_event = { id = india_events.2 months = 3 } }
	}
}

india_events.2 = { # Delhi Durbar
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:delhi_scope

	duration = 3

	title = india_events.2.t
	desc = india_events.2.d
	flavor = india_events.2.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_cross_cultural_festival"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by previous event
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			top_overlord = {
				NOT = {
					has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_DELHI
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				is_subject = yes
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		s:STATE_DELHI = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = ROOT
				}
				save_scope_as = delhi_scope
			}
		}
		top_overlord = {
			ruler = {
				save_scope_as = monarch_scope
			}
		}
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = viceroy_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_delhi_durbar
			days = normal_modifier_time
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_delhi_durbar_approval
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.2.b
		trigger = {
			any_political_movement = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_delhi_durbar
			days = normal_modifier_time
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = indian_independence_request_tt
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_indian_independence_request
				days = long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

india_events.3 = { # Koh-i-Noor
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:punjab_scope

	duration = 3

	title = india_events.3.t
	desc = india_events.3.d
	flavor = india_events.3.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_redcoats"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by on_action
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		is_subject = no
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = india_scope
		s:STATE_PUNJAB = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						owner = ROOT
						owner = {
							is_subject_of = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = punjab_scope
			}
		}
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = overlord_scope
		}
	}

	option = { # Send it to Britain
		name = india_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:overlord_scope = {
			post_notification = koh_i_noor_sent
			set_variable = koh_i_noor
		}
		add_small_indian_radicals = yes
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_dispatched_koh_i_noor_diamond
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = top_overlord
			value = 10
		}
	}

	option = { # Keep it in India
		name = india_events.3.b
		scope:overlord_scope = {
			post_notification = koh_i_noor_not_sent
		}
		add_small_indian_loyalists = yes
		change_relations = {
			country = top_overlord
			value = -5
		}
	}
}

india_events.4 = { # Demanding return of the Koh-i-Noor
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = india_events.4.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_events.4.d_1
				trigger = {
					scope:india_scope = {
						OR = {
							c:BHT ?= this
							c:BIC ?= this
						}
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_events.4.d_2
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = india_events.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_telegraph_dictate"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by decision
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = koh_i_noor_owner_scope
		var:koh_i_noor_decision_user ?= {
			save_scope_as = india_scope
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			exists = scope:india_scope
		}
	}

	option = { # No.
		name = india_events.4.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:india_scope = {
			post_notification = koh_i_noor_not_returned
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:india_scope
			value = -10
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_patriotic
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_refused_to_surrender_diamond
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Yes.
		name = india_events.4.b
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = returned_koh_i_noor_tt
			remove_variable = koh_i_noor
		}
		scope:india_scope = {
			post_notification = koh_i_noor_returned
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:india_scope
			value = 25
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_patriotic
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_surrendered_diamond
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

india_events.5 = { # Deccan Riots
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:riots_site_scope

	duration = 3

	title = india_events.5.t
	desc = india_events.5.d
	flavor = india_events.5.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_protest"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		c:BIC ?= this
		any_scope_state = {
			OR = {
				state_region = s:STATE_KURNOOL
				state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
				state_region = s:STATE_HYDERABAD
				state_region = s:STATE_MYSORE
			}
			OR = {
				turmoil > 0.20
				obstinance >= 0.10
			}
			average_sol < average_expected_sol
			any_scope_pop = {
				OR = {
					is_pop_type = peasants
					is_pop_type = slaves
				}
				standard_of_living < state.average_expected_sol
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_KURNOOL
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
					state_region = s:STATE_HYDERABAD
					state_region = s:STATE_MYSORE
				}
				OR = {
					turmoil > 0.20
					obstinance >= 0.10
				}
				average_sol < average_expected_sol
				any_scope_pop = {
					OR = {
						is_pop_type = peasants
						is_pop_type = slaves
					}
					standard_of_living < state.average_expected_sol
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = riots_site_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.5.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:riots_site_scope = {
			state_region = {
				add_devastation = 25
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_deccan_riots_unaffected
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.5.b
		scope:riots_site_scope = {
			state_region = {
				add_devastation = 10
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = slaves_freed_in_state_tt
				every_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						is_pop_type = slaves
					}
					change_poptype = pop_type:laborers
				}
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = large_radicals
				pop_type = aristocrats
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = large_radicals
				pop_type = shopkeepers
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_deccan_riots_limited_money_lending
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_deccan_riots_limited_money_lending
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

india_events.6 = { # Hindu-German Conspiracy
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = india_events.6.t
	desc = india_events.6.d
	flavor = india_events.6.f

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_conspiring"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		any_political_movement = {
			is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
			movement_is_causing_obstinance = yes
		}
		is_subject = yes
		top_overlord = {
			any_enemy_in_war = {
				country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
				OR = {
					has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_bengal
					has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_madras
					has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_bombay
					has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_india
					has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_punjab
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		top_overlord = {
			ordered_enemy_in_war = {
				limit = {
					country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_bengal
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_madras
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_bombay
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_india
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_punjab
					}
				}
				order_by = country_rank
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
				save_scope_as = enemy_great_power_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = overlord_scope
		}
		save_scope_as = india_scope
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			is_subject_of = scope:overlord_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.6.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:overlord_scope = {
			post_notification = combatting_hindu_german_conspiracy
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:overlord_scope
			value = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = conspiracy_trials_in_30_days_tt
			trigger_event = { id = india_events.7 days = 30 }
		}
		ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_revolutionary_conspiracy_crackdown
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_revolutionary_conspiracy_crackdown
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.6.b
		scope:overlord_scope = {
			post_notification = not_combatting_hindu_german_conspiracy
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:overlord_scope
			value = -10
		}
		add_small_indian_radicals = yes
	}
}

india_events.7 = { # Lahore Trials
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:conspiracy_trial_state

	duration = 3

	title = india_events.7.t
	desc = india_events.7.d
	flavor = india_events.7.f

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_prison"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by previous event
		is_subject = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_PUNJAB
				}
			}
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_PUNJAB
				}
				save_scope_as = conspiracy_trial_state
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
						state_region = s:STATE_MADRAS
						state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = conspiracy_trial_state
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			is_subject = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Good riddance.
		name = india_events.7.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_indian_conspiracy_trials
			days = normal_modifier_time
			is_decaying = yes
			multiplier = 1.5
		}
	}

	option = { # Use the trials as a platform for independence.
		name = india_events.7.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_indian_conspiracy_trials
			days = normal_modifier_time
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_india_forty_two_martyrs
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

			p:xE0EFA0.state.owner = ROOT
		}
	}

	option = { # Impressive, very nice.
		name = india_events.8.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:phool_walon_ki_sair_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_phool_walon_ki_sair
				months = 12
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Suppress it for nationalism.
		name = india_events.8.b
		trigger = {
			any_primary_culture = {
				has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european
			}
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_shuttered_pwks
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:phool_walon_ki_sair_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				religion = rel:hindu
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				religion = rel:sunni
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Encourage it.
		name = india_events.8.c
		add_treasury = -10000
		scope:phool_walon_ki_sair_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_phool_walon_ki_sair
				months = 12
				multiplier = 1.5
			}
		}
	}
}

		c:BIC ?= this

		NOT = {
			has_government_type = gov_chartered_company
		}
		has_technology_researched = military_statistics
	}

	immediate = {
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			save_scope_as = government_scope
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			save_scope_as = presidency_armies
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.9.a

			set_interest_group_name = ig_indian_army

		}
	}

			}

			set_interest_group_name = ig_indian_army
		}
		add_small_indian_loyalists = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_encouraged_indian_military_participation

		}
	}

india_events.12 = { # Anne Besant goes to India
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	duration = 3

	title = india_events.12.t
	desc = india_events.12.d
	flavor = india_events.12.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	left_icon = scope:besant_scope

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		any_country = {
			OR = {
				c:BIC ?= this
				c:BHT ?= this
			}
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_MADRAS
			}
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = GBR_annie_besant
		}
		NOT = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_multicultural # To prevent this event from stealing Besant whilst you're enacting Multiculturalism with her help.
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		}
		game_date > 1893.1.1
	}

	immediate = {
		ordered_country = { # Just in case multiple countries own this state
			limit = {
				OR = {
					c:BIC ?= this
					c:BHT ?= this
				}
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_MADRAS
				}
			}
			order_by = gdp
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = india_scope
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_MADRAS
				}
				save_scope_as = madras_state
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = GBR_annie_besant
			}
			save_scope_as = besant_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.12.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
		scope:india_scope = {
			post_notification = besant_to_india
		}
		scope:besant_scope = {
			transfer_character = scope:india_scope
		}
	}

	option = { # Nothing ever happens.
		name = india_events.12.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		capital = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_london_theosophical_society
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

			}

			limit = {
				has_template = GBR_annie_besant
			}
			save_scope_as = besant_scope

		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = krishnamurti_var
			}
			save_scope_as = krishnamurti_scope
		}
		set_variable = krishnamurti_spawn
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.13.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

	option = {

		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
		}
		custom_tooltip = krishnamurti_chain_end_tt
		remove_global_variable = theosophy_var
	}
}

	placement = scope:star_camp_state

	desc = india_events.16.d
	flavor = india_events.16.f

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		any_country = {
			OR = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_europe
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_north_america
			}
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_total_separation
		}
		game_date > 1925.1.1
		has_global_variable = theosophy_var
		global_var:theosophy_var = 3
	}

	immediate = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_europe
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_north_america
				}
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_total_separation
			}
			ordered_scope_state = {
				order_by = gdp
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
				save_scope_as = star_camp_state
			}
		}
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = krishnamurti_var
			}
			save_scope_as = krishnamurti_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.16.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			scope:krishnamurti_scope = {
				popularity > 15
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
		}
		remove_global_variable = theosophy_var
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = krishnamurti_exile_tt
			scope:krishnamurti_scope = {
				add_character_role = agitator
				remove_character_role = politician
				free_character_from_void = yes
				exile_character = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.16.b
		trigger = {
			scope:krishnamurti_scope = {
				popularity > 15
			}
			empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
		remove_global_variable = theosophy_var
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = krishnamurti_available_tt
			scope:krishnamurti_scope = {
				add_character_role = agitator
				remove_character_role = politician
				hidden_effect = {
					free_character_from_void = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.16.c
		trigger = {
			scope:krishnamurti_scope = {
				popularity <= 15
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
		remove_global_variable = theosophy_var
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = krishnamurti_retirement_tt
			scope:krishnamurti_scope = {
				kill_character = {
					hidden = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

india_events.18 = { # Sale of Berar
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = india_events.18.t
	desc = india_events.18.d
	flavor = india_events.18.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_countryside"
	}

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# triggered by decision
	}

	immediate = {
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = bic_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_CENTRAL_PROVINCES
			}
			save_scope_as = berar_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.18.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
		}
		scope:berar_scope = {
			set_state_owner = c:BIC
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = lease_of_berar_income
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = lease_of_berar_costs
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_events.18.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
		create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
			type = catalyst_event_negative
			target = c:BIC
		}
	}

﻿namespace = india_nationalism_events

india_nationalism_events.1 = { # The Anarchy
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	duration = 3

	title = india_nationalism_events.1.t
	desc = india_nationalism_events.1.d
	flavor = india_nationalism_events.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_devastation"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = india_scope
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					is_ruler = yes
					is_heir = yes
				}
				NOT = {
					has_socialist_ideology = yes
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = british_politician_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.1.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 75
		}
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = every_indian_subject_made_independent_tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:BIC
					c:BIC ?= {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
				c:BIC ?= {
					trigger_event = { id = india_nationalism_events.2 popup = yes }
				}
			}
			else = {
				every_subject_or_below = {
					limit = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
						NOR = {
							c:BIC ?= this
							c:BCE ?= this
						}
					}
					make_independent = yes
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = european_pops_may_flee_tt
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			strata = lower
		}
	}
}

india_nationalism_events.2 = { # The Anarchy - 2 - British Withdrawal
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	duration = 3

	title = india_nationalism_events.2.t
	desc = india_nationalism_events.2.d
	flavor = india_nationalism_events.2.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_devastation"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = overlord_scope
		}
		random_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = {
					is_homeland = cu:british
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = evacuation_state
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
			}
			save_scope_as = calcutta_scope
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				civil_war_origin_country ?= ROOT
			}
			save_scope_as = revolution_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = most_european_pops_abandon_india_tt
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european
					}
				}
				random_list = {
					75 = {
						move_pop = scope:evacuation_state
					}
					25 = {

					}
				}
			}
		}
		make_independent = yes
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_revolution
			}
			end_play = yes
		}
		collapse_india_effect = yes
		change_tag = BGL
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_primary_culture = cu:british
			add_primary_culture = cu:bengali
			set_state_religion = rel:sunni
			state_religion_switch_effect = yes
			every_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						culture = cu:british
						culture = cu:scottish
					}
				}
				kill_character = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.2.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 35
		}
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:revolution_scope
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = most_european_pops_abandon_india_tt
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european
					}
				}
				random_list = {
					75 = {
						move_pop = scope:evacuation_state
					}
					25 = {

					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_player = yes
			}
			play_as = scope:revolution_scope
		}
		make_independent = yes
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_revolution
			}
			end_play = yes
		}
		scope:revolution_scope = {
			annex = ROOT
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.2.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
		trigger = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
			}
		}
		set_capital = STATE_WEST_BENGAL
		set_variable = bengal_free_state
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = some_european_pops_abandon_india_tt
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european
					}
				}
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						move_pop = scope:evacuation_state
					}
					75 = {
						move_pop = owner.capital
					}
				}
			}
		}
		make_independent = yes
		custom_tooltip = bengal_free_state_tt
		set_variable = bic_collapse_var
		collapse_india_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_neighbouring_state = {
						owner = root
						state_region = s:STATE_BIHAR
					}
				}
				s:STATE_BIHAR.region_state:BIC ?= {
					set_state_owner = PREV
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_ASSAM
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:ASM
					}
					s:STATE_ASSAM.region_state:BIC ?= {
						set_state_owner = c:ASM
					}
				}
				else = {
					create_country = {
						tag = ASM
						origin = root
						state = s:STATE_ASSAM.region_state:BIC
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_large_indian_radicals = yes
	}
}

india_nationalism_events.3 = { # The Salt March
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	duration = 3

	title = india_nationalism_events.3.t
	desc = india_nationalism_events.3.d
	flavor = india_nationalism_events.3.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_protest"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		c:BIC ?= this
		any_scope_state = {
			is_coastal = yes
			is_incorporated = yes
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			culture = {
				has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
			}
			has_role = agitator
		}
		any_political_lobby = {
			is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_GUJARAT
				}
			}
			s:STATE_GUJARAT.region_state:BIC = {
				save_scope_as = salt_march_state
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					is_coastal = yes
					is_incorporated = yes
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
				}
				save_scope_as = salt_march_state
			}
		}
		ordered_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				culture = {
					has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
				}
				has_role = agitator
			}
			order_by = popularity
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = prisoner_scope
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
			}
			save_scope_as = anti_overlord_lobby
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:prisoner_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_salt_march_leader
				days = very_long_modifier_time
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = to_jail_5_tt
				set_variable = { name = prison_time_var value = 60 }
				set_variable = { name = prison_country value = owner }
				place_character_in_void = 72
			}
		}
		scope:salt_march_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_salt_tax_protests
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.3.b
		scope:salt_march_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_salt_tax_protests
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

india_nationalism_events.4 = { # Gandhi in Sooth Efrika
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:gandhi_state

	duration = 3

	title = india_nationalism_events.4.t
	desc = india_nationalism_events.4.d
	flavor = india_nationalism_events.4.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		game_date >= 1893.1.1
		c:SAF ?= this
		any_scope_state = {
			OR = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ZULULAND
				state_region = s:STATE_EASTERN_CAPE
			}
			any_scope_pop = {
				culture = {
					has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
				}
			}
		}
		top_overlord = {
			any_subject_or_below = {
				OR = {
					c:BIC ?= this
					c:BHT ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = gandhi_spawn
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = gandhi_spawn
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ZULULAND
				}
			}
			s:STATE_ZULULAND.region_state:SAF ?= {
				save_scope_as = gandhi_state
			}
		}
		else = {
			s:STATE_EASTERN_CAPE.region_state:SAF ?= {
				save_scope_as = gandhi_state
			}
		}
		create_character = {
			template = BIC_mohandas_karamchand_gandhi
			on_created = {
				set_variable = gandhi_var
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = gandhi_var
			}
			save_scope_as = gandhi_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.4.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:gandhi_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_natal_indian_congress
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.4.b
		scope:gandhi_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_natal_indian_congress
				days = long_modifier_time
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
		}
		scope:gandhi_scope = {
			add_trait = scarred
		}
	}
}

india_nationalism_events.5 = { # Gandhi in India
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	duration = 3

	title = india_nationalism_events.5.t
	desc = india_nationalism_events.5.d
	flavor = india_nationalism_events.5.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	left_icon = scope:gandhi_scope

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		c:BIC ?= this
		game_date >= 1910.1.1
		is_subject = yes
		NOT = {
			any_country = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_template = BIC_mohandas_karamchand_gandhi
				}
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_multicultural
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = gandhi_arrived
		}
		any_political_movement = {
			is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = gandhi_arrived
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BIC_mohandas_karamchand_gandhi
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BIC_mohandas_karamchand_gandhi
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = gandhi_exit_scope
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = BIC_mohandas_karamchand_gandhi
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = BIC_mohandas_karamchand_gandhi
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = BIC_mohandas_karamchand_gandhi
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = BIC_mohandas_karamchand_gandhi
				on_created = {
					set_variable = gandhi_var
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = BIC_mohandas_karamchand_gandhi
			}
			save_scope_as = gandhi_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:gandhi_exit_scope
			}
			scope:gandhi_exit_scope = {
				post_notification = gandhi_leaves
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.5.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:gandhi_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_father_of_satyagraha
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.5.b
		scope:gandhi_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_father_of_satyagraha
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
		}
	}
}

						NOT = {

				}

		c:GBR ?= this

			OR = {
				c:BIC ?= this
				c:BHT ?= this
			}
		}
		OR = {
			any_scope_character = {
				OR = {
					has_socialist_ideology = yes
					has_progressive_ideology = yes
				}
			}
			exists = py:social_democrat_party
			exists = py:communist_party

			has_variable = india_league
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_subject_or_below = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					c:BIC ?= this
					c:BHT ?= this
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = india_scope
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_socialist_ideology = yes
					has_progressive_ideology = yes
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = india_league_character
		}
		random_active_party = {
			limit = {
				party_has_members = yes
				OR = {
					is_party_type = social_democrat_party
					is_party_type = communist_party
					is_party_type = anarchist_party

			}
			save_scope_as = india_league_party

			}
			scope:india_league_party = {
				random_member = {
					add_modifier = {
						name = modifier_india_league_ig
						days = normal_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:india_league_character
			}
			scope:india_league_character = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_india_league_character
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_nationalism_events.6.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:india_league_party
			}
			scope:india_league_party = {
				random_member = {
					add_modifier = {
						name = modifier_india_league_ig
						days = normal_modifier_time
						multiplier = 0.5
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:india_league_character
			}
			scope:india_league_character = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_india_league_character
					days = normal_modifier_time
					multiplier = 0.5
				}
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = medium_radicals
			pop_type = officers
			culture = cu:british
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = medium_radicals
			pop_type = officers
			culture = cu:scottish
		}
	}

		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_party = yes
						party ?= {
							OR = {
								is_party_type = communist_party
								is_party_type = social_democrat_party
								is_party_type = anarchist_party
							}
						}
					}
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_socialist_ideology = yes
							has_progressive_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_condition_of_india_rejected
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

namespace = india_non_cooperation

india_non_cooperation.1 = { # The Non-Cooperation Movement
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	duration = 3

	title = india_non_cooperation.1.t
	desc = india_non_cooperation.1.d
	flavor = india_non_cooperation.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ip2_india_protest.bk2"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	immediate = {
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = india_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				root.market = top_overlord.market
			}
			market = {
				ordered_market_goods = {
					limit = {
						is_tradeable = yes
						market_goods_sell_orders > 200
					}
					order_by = "market.market_production_share(scope:probably_britain_scope)"
					position = 0
					check_range_bounds = no
					goods = {
						save_scope_as = british_trade_route_goods
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			market = {
				ordered_market_goods = {
					limit = {
						is_tradeable = yes
						market_goods_sell_orders > 200
					}
					order_by = "market.market_imports_reliance(scope:probably_britain_scope.market)"
					position = 0
					check_range_bounds = no
					goods = {
						save_scope_as = british_trade_route_goods	# need a good to be called on for loc in the non-trade-route path
					}
				}
			}
		}
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = probably_britain_scope
			trigger_event = { id = india_non_cooperation.2 }
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
				movement_is_causing_obstinance = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = indian_independence_movement

			if = {
				limit = {
					any_supporting_character = {
						# repeating the requirements for sending someone to jail
						is_in_exile_pool = no
						is_in_void = no
						is_ruler = no
						is_heir = no
						has_role = agitator
						is_immortal = no
					}
				}
				random_supporting_character = {
					limit = {
						is_in_exile_pool = no
						is_in_void = no
						is_ruler = no
						is_heir = no
						has_role = agitator
						is_immortal = no
					}
					save_scope_as = indian_independence_movement_agitator
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_non_cooperation.1.a
		default_option = yes

		if = {
			limit = { has_journal_entry = je_india_home_rule }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = india_bar_advance_10_tt
				je:je_india_home_rule ?= {
					add_progress = { value = 10 name = je_india_home_rule_progressbar }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_small_indian_radicals = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_non_cooperation.1.b
		highlighted_option = yes

		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties }
			exists = scope:indian_independence_movement_agitator
		}

		scope:indian_independence_movement_agitator = {
			save_scope_as = prisoner_scope
		}
		trigger_event = { id = jail.1 popup = yes }
	}
}

india_non_cooperation.3 = { # Hartal!
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:hartal_state

	duration = 3

	title = india_non_cooperation.3.t
	desc = india_non_cooperation.3.d
	flavor = india_non_cooperation.3.f

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ip2_india_factory.bk2"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_industry.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_scope_state = {
			NOT = { has_modifier = hartal_action }
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ordered_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_modifier = hartal_action }
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
			}
			order_by = gdp
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = hartal_state
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
			}
			save_scope_as = indian_independence_movement
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_non_cooperation.3.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:hartal_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = hartal_action
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}


		custom_tooltip = {	# not using the scripted effect because this is targeting a specific state
			text = add_small_indian_radicals_tt
			every_scope_culture = {
				limit = {
					has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
				}
				scope:hartal_state = {
					add_radicals = {
						value = small_radicals
						culture = prev
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_non_cooperation.3.b

		scope:hartal_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = hartal_action
				days = short_modifier_time
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
		}

		scope:indian_independence_movement = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = hartal_negotiating_with_leaders
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

india_non_cooperation.5 = { # The Chauri Chaura Incident / The Blazing Chowki
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:chauri_chaura_state

	duration = 3

	title = india_non_cooperation.5.t
	desc = india_non_cooperation.5.d
	flavor = india_non_cooperation.5.f

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ip2_intercommunal_tension.bk2"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"

	# triggered by radicalism failure of JE

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			save_scope_as = chauri_chaura_state
		}
		random_scope_culture = {
			limit = {
				has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
				has_homeland = prev
			}
			save_scope_as = firebrand_movement_leader
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_india_pan_national
			}
			save_scope_as = indian_independence_movement

			ordered_influenced_interest_group = {
				order_by = ig_clout
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
				save_scope_as = powerful_movement_ig
			}

			random_supporting_character = {
				limit = {
					is_in_exile_pool = no
					is_in_void = no
					is_ruler = no
					is_heir = no
					OR = {
						has_role = agitator
						has_role = politician
					}
					OR = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pacifist
					}
					is_immortal = no
				}
				save_scope_as = nonviolent_movement_leader
				interest_group = {
					save_scope_as = nonviolent_movement_leader_ig
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_non_cooperation.5.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:indian_independence_movement = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = failure_of_non_cooperation
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}

		scope:chauri_chaura_state = {
			state_region = {
				add_devastation = 10
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = india_non_cooperation.5.b
		trigger = { exists = scope:nonviolent_movement_leader }

		custom_tooltip = indian_nationalist_leader_retires_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:nonviolent_movement_leader = {
					has_role = agitator
					NOT = { has_role = politician }
				}
			}
			scope:nonviolent_movement_leader = {
				hidden_effect = {
					kill_character = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { empty_agitator_slots > 0 }
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = an_agitator_appears_tt
					create_character = {
						interest_group = scope:powerful_movement_ig
		    			ideology = ideology_sovereignist_leader
		    			is_agitator = yes
		    			culture = scope:firebrand_movement_leader
					    trait_generation = {
							if = {
								limit = {
									trait_value < 3
								}
								add_trait = demagogue
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									trait_value < 3
								}
								add_trait = charismatic
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									trait_value < 3
								}
								add_trait = ambitious
							}
					    }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:nonviolent_movement_leader = {
					has_role = politician
				}
			}
			scope:nonviolent_movement_leader = {
				hidden_effect = {
					kill_character = yes
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				ig_leader = yes
				interest_group = scope:nonviolent_movement_leader_ig
    			ideology = ideology_sovereignist_leader
				culture = scope:firebrand_movement_leader
			    trait_generation = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							trait_value < 3
						}
						add_trait = demagogue
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							trait_value < 3
						}
						add_trait = charismatic
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							trait_value < 3
						}
						add_trait = ambitious
					}
			    }
    		}
    	}

		scope:chauri_chaura_state = {
			add_devastation = 10
		}
	}
}

﻿namespace = india_railway

# Train Robbery
india_railway.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:relevant_state
	title = india_railway.1.t
	desc = india_railway.1.d
	flavor = india_railway.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_trains"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by Purveyor of Progres JE
		is_subject = yes
		liberty_desire >= 25
		any_scope_state = {
			has_building = building_railway
			NOT = { has_variable = railway_robbery_happened }
			NOT = { has_variable = railway_trial_completed }
			any_scope_pop = {
				pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
			}
			any_scope_pop = {
				pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
				culture = {
					has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_building = building_railway
				NOT = { has_variable = railway_robbery_happened }
				NOT = { has_variable = railway_trial_completed }
				any_scope_pop = {
					pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
				}
				any_scope_pop = {
					pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
					culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone
					}
				}
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
				}
				save_scope_as = discriminated_pop
				culture = { save_scope_as = discriminated_culture }
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
					culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = not_discriminated_pop
					culture = { save_scope_as = not_discriminated_culture }
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		is_subject = no
	}

	option = {
		name = india_railway.1.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = do_control_the_press
			days = normal_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_very_small
		}
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				culture = scope:not_discriminated_culture
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_variable = railway_robbery_happened
			}
		}

	}
	option = {
		name = india_railway.1.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = not_control_the_press
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		scope:relevant_state = {
			hidden_effect = {
				set_variable = railway_robbery_happened
			}
		}
	}
}

# Stealing From The Crown
india_railway.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = india_railway.2.t
	desc = india_railway.2.d
	flavor = india_railway.2.f

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_hostile_court"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by Purveyor of Progres JE
		any_scope_state = {
			has_building = building_railway
			has_variable = railway_robbery_happened
			NOT = { has_variable = railway_trial_completed }
			any_scope_pop = {
				pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_building = building_railway
				has_variable = railway_robbery_happened
				NOT = { has_variable = railway_trial_completed }
				any_scope_pop = {
					pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
				}
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
				}
				save_scope_as = discriminated_pop
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}

	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		is_subject = no
	}

	option = { # Interrogate for more evidence
		name = india_railway.2.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = railway_trial_authority
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = large_radicals
				culture = scope:discriminated_pop.culture
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = railway_robbery_happened
				set_variable = railway_trial_completed
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = india_railway.2.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = railway_trial_legit
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				culture = scope:discriminated_pop.culture
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = railway_robbery_happened
				set_variable = railway_trial_completed
			}
		}
	}
}

# A Regional Famine
india_railway.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:relevant_state
	title = india_railway.3.t
	desc = india_railway.3.d
	flavor = india_railway.3.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_countryside"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by Purveyor of Progres JE
		is_subject = yes
		any_scope_state = {
			has_building = building_railway
			any_scope_pop = {
				pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
			}
			has_famine = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_building = building_railway
				any_scope_pop = {
					pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
				}
				has_famine = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}

	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		is_subject = no
	}

	option = {
		name = india_railway.3.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = railway_famine
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = railway_hunger_relief
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_individualist
					leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_market_liberal }
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = railway_ig_disapproval
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}

	}
	option = {
		name = india_railway.3.b
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = railway_famine
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = railway_hunger_no_relief
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_individualist
					leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_market_liberal }
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = railway_ig_approval
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# First-Class Accoutrements
india_railway.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = india_railway.4.t
	desc = india_railway.4.d
	flavor = india_railway.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_intercommunal_tensions"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by Purveyor of Progres JE
		is_subject = yes
		any_scope_state = {
			has_building = building_railway
			any_scope_pop = {
				pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
			}
			any_scope_pop = {
				pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
				culture = {
					has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_building = building_railway
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
				}
				save_scope_as = discriminated_pop
				culture = { save_scope_as = discriminated_culture }
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
					culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = not_discriminated_pop
				culture = { save_scope_as = not_discriminated_culture }
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		is_subject = no
	}

	option = {
		name = india_railway.4.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				culture = scope:not_discriminated_pop.culture
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = rising_intracommunal_tension
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = india_railway.4.b
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				culture = scope:discriminated_pop.culture
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				culture = scope:not_discriminated_pop.culture
			}
		}
	}
}

# Railways for Prosperity
india_railway.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = india_railway.5.t
	desc = india_railway.5.d
	flavor = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_railway.5.f
				trigger = {
					is_subject = yes
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = india_railway.5.f2
				trigger = {
					is_subject = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_trains"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by Purveyor of Progres JE
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_subject = yes
			}
			top_overlord = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_overlord
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
	}

	option = {
		name = india_railway.5.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:relevant_state
			}
			scope:relevant_state = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = india_railway_reward
					days = long_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			ordered_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_bengal
						region = sr:region_bombay
						region = sr:region_central_india
						region = sr:region_madras
						region = sr:region_punjab
					}
				}
				order_by = gdp
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
				add_modifier = {
					name = india_railway_reward
					days = long_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = jail

jail.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = jail.1.t
	desc = jail.1.d
	flavor = jail.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_prison"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by interaction
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:prisoner_scope
			}
			scope:prisoner_scope = {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = jail.1.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:prisoner_scope.political_movement
			}
			scope:prisoner_scope.political_movement ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_jailed_movement_agitator
					days = normal_modifier_time
					multiplier = jail_three_year_value
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			owner = {
				add_radicals = {
					value = 0.01
					interest_group = scope:prisoner_scope.interest_group
				}
			}
		}
		scope:prisoner_scope = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = to_jail_3_tt
				set_variable = { name = prison_time_var value = 36 }
				set_variable = { name = prison_country value = owner }
				place_character_in_void = 48
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = jail.1.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:prisoner_scope.political_movement
			}
			scope:prisoner_scope.political_movement ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_jailed_movement_agitator
					days = normal_modifier_time
					multiplier = jail_five_year_value
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			owner = {
				add_radicals = {
					value = 0.03
					interest_group = scope:prisoner_scope.interest_group
				}
			}
		}
		scope:prisoner_scope = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = to_jail_5_tt
				set_variable = { name = prison_time_var value = 60 }
				set_variable = { name = prison_country value = owner }
				place_character_in_void = 72
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = jail.1.c
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:prisoner_scope.political_movement
			}
			scope:prisoner_scope.political_movement ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_jailed_movement_agitator
					days = normal_modifier_time
					multiplier = jail_ten_year_value
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			owner = {
				add_radicals = {
					value = 0.05
					interest_group = scope:prisoner_scope.interest_group
				}
			}
		}
		scope:prisoner_scope = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = to_jail_10_tt
				set_variable = { name = prison_time_var value = 120 }
				set_variable = { name = prison_country value = owner }
				place_character_in_void = 132
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = jail.1.e
		custom_tooltip = to_jail_exonerated_tt
	}
}
﻿namespace = mughal

mughal.1 = { # Heiring Grievances
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = mughal.1.t
	desc = mughal.1.d
	flavor = mughal.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "asia_politician_parliament_motion"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = long_modifier_time } # the chances of this happening twice are slim, but Akbar insisting again years later sounds appropriate for him.

	trigger = {
		is_subject = yes
		ruler = {
			has_template = mug_akbar_ii
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			is_heir = yes
			has_template = mug_bahadur_ii
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			ruler = {
				NOT = { has_template = mug_akbar_ii } # event cancels if Akbar dithered and didn't press the succession issue either way
			}
			is_subject = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		overlord = {
			save_scope_as = probably_east_india_company_scope
		}
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = probably_britain_scope
		}
		root = {
			save_scope_as = hindustan
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = { has_template = mug_akbar_ii }
			save_scope_as = akbar_ii
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = { has_template = mug_bahadur_ii }
			save_scope_as = bahadur_ii
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mughal.1.a
		default_option = yes

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}

		add_liberty_desire = -5
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = mirza_salim_will_probably_die
			trigger_event = {
				id = mughal.2
				months = 3
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mughal.1.b

		ai_chance = {
			base = 0

			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						has_attitude = {
							who = scope:probably_east_india_company_scope
							attitude = DEFIANT
						}
						has_attitude = {
							who = scope:probably_east_india_company_scope
							attitude = REBELLIOUS
						}
					}
				}
				add = 10
			}
		}

		add_modifier = {
			name = assertive_mughals
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = belated_british_interference
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		overlord = {
			change_relations = {
				country = root
				value = -50
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = mirza_salim_succeeds
			create_character = {
				template = mug_mirza_salim
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = {
				id = mughal.3 # separate event to fire BIC notification, so Mirza Salim can be scoped to in loc properly
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
