mughal.6 = { # The British India Society
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = mughal.6.t
	desc = mughal.6.d
	flavor = mughal.6.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_persian_court"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		overlord = {
			save_scope_as = probably_east_india_company_scope
		}
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = probably_britain_scope
		}

		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = hindustan_ruler
		}

		root = {
			save_scope_as = hindustan
			ruler = {
				save_scope_as = hindustan_ruler
			}

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_slavery_banned }
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_serfdom
				}
			}
			i:ideology_abolitionist = {
				save_scope_as = potentially_swayed_mug_ideology
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_role = politician
					is_ruler = no # but it is okay if an heir is targeted with this
					OR = {
						is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
						is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
						is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
						is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists
						is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
					}
					NOT = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_abolitionist }
				}
				save_scope_as = potentially_swayed_mug_politician
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_slavery_banned
				NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_serfdom }
				has_technology_researched = egalitarianism
			}
			i:ideology_radical = {
				save_scope_as = potentially_swayed_mug_ideology
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_role = politician
					is_ruler = no # but it is okay if an heir is targeted with this
					OR = {
						is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk
						is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
						is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
						is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
						is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists
					}
					NOT = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical }
				}
				save_scope_as = potentially_swayed_mug_politician
			}
		}
		else = {
			i:ideology_abolitionist = {
				save_scope_as = potentially_swayed_mug_ideology
			}
		}

		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				}
				target = scope:probably_britain_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = pro_probably_britain_lobby_scope
		}

		set_variable = engaged_with_british_india_society
	}

	trigger = {
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		is_subject = yes
		exists = overlord
		exists = top_overlord
		overlord = {
			is_subject = yes
			is_direct_subject_of = top_overlord
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = engaged_with_british_india_society
		}
		OR = {
			overlord = {
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:british
			}
			top_overlord = {
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:british
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { exists = scope:probably_east_india_company_scope }
			NOT = { exists = scope:probably_britain_scope }
			NOT = { scope:probably_east_india_company_scope = { is_direct_subject_of = scope:probably_britain_scope } }
		}
		empty_agitator_slots = 0
	}

	option = {
		name = mughal.6.a
        default_option = yes

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1

			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					any_scope_character = {
						is_ruler = yes
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_slaver
					}
				}
				add = -1
			}
		}

		create_character = {
			template = mug_george_thompson

			# if MUG does not have any laws an abolitionist would object to, George Thompson will be radical instead
			on_created = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						owner = {
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_slavery_banned
							NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_serfdom }
							has_technology_researched = egalitarianism
						}
					}
					set_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
				}
			}
		}

		add_modifier = {
			name = british_india_society_support # +0.05 liberty desire, but for a very long time
			days = very_long_modifier_time
		}

		random_list = {
			1 = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = mughal_6_slavers_offended
					every_interest_group = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pro_slavery
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pro_slavery_colonial
								leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_slaver }
							}
						}
						add_modifier = {
							name = ig_slavers_mad
							days = short_modifier_time
							multiplier = 0.66
						}
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:potentially_swayed_mug_politician
					}
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = mughal_6_moderate_swayed_to_ideology

						scope:potentially_swayed_mug_politician = {
							set_ideology = scope:potentially_swayed_mug_ideology
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:potentially_swayed_mug_ideology
						any_interest_group = {
							leader = { has_ideology = scope:potentially_swayed_mug_ideology }
						}
					}
					every_interest_group = {
						limit = {
							leader = { has_ideology = scope:potentially_swayed_mug_ideology }
						}
						add_modifier = {
							name = ideology_surge_in_popularity
							days = short_modifier_time
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
						add_modifier = {
							name = ideology_surge_in_popularity
							days = short_modifier_time
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mughal.6.b

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					OR = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pro_slavery
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pro_slavery_colonial
						leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_slaver }
					}
				}
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pro_slavery
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pro_slavery_colonial
						leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_slaver }
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = ig_mug_slavers_pleased
					days = short_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			ig:ig_landowners ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = ig_mug_slavers_pleased
					days = short_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		custom_tooltip = mughal_6_overlord_pleased_at_discretion
		overlord = {
			change_relations = {
				country = root
				value = 25
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mughal.6.c
		highlighted_option = yes

		trigger = {
			has_dlc_feature = lobbies
			exists = scope:pro_probably_britain_lobby_scope
		}

		add_modifier = {
			name = british_india_society_support
			days = very_long_modifier_time
			multiplier = 2
		}

		scope:probably_east_india_company_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = britains_baleful_eye		# decreases LD for BIC and increases LD for ALL BIC subjects
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
			post_notification = britain_hindustan_intervention
		}

		scope:pro_probably_britain_lobby_scope = { # Well done, lads!
			change_appeasement = {
			   amount = 2
			   factor = appeasement_lobby_pleased
			}
		}
	}
}

mughal.7 = { # The Peacock Throne Flies the Coop!
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = mughal.7.t
	desc = mughal.7.d
	flavor = mughal.7.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_cross_cultural_festival"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	immediate = {
		root = {
			ruler = { save_scope_as = hindustan_ruler }
			save_scope_as = mughal_scope
		}

		set_variable = {
			name = mughal_annex_count
			value = 0
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mughal.7.a
        default_option = yes

        # give MUG the primary cultures it used to have prior to this content
        add_primary_culture = cu:avadhi
        add_primary_culture = cu:bengali
        add_primary_culture = cu:sindi
        add_primary_culture = cu:kannada

    	custom_tooltip = {
    		text = mughal_7_annex_up_to_3_immediate_neighbors

	    	if = {
	    		limit = {
					any_neighbouring_state = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
						owner = {
							is_player = no
							relations:root >= 20
							OR = {
								is_subject = no
								is_subject_of = scope:bic_scope
							}
							any_primary_culture = {
								shares_heritage_trait_group_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
	    		}
	    		while = {
	    			limit = {
		    			var:mughal_annex_count < 3
		    		}
					ordered_country = {
						limit = {
							relations:root >= define:NDiplomacy|RELATIONS_THRESHOLD_CORDIAL
							is_player = no
							OR = {
								is_subject = no
								is_subject_of = scope:bic_scope
							}
							any_primary_culture = {
								shares_heritage_trait_group_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT
							}
							any_scope_state = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
								any_neighbouring_state = {
									owner = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						order_by = gdp
						position = 0
						check_range_bounds = no
						save_temporary_scope_as = to_be_annexed
					}

					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:to_be_annexed
							exists = scope:bic_scope
							scope:to_be_annexed = { is_subject_of = scope:bic_scope }
						}
						scope:bic_scope = {
							change_relations = {
								country = root
								value = -10
							}
						}
					}

					if = {
						limit = { exists = scope:to_be_annexed }
						annex = scope:to_be_annexed

						change_variable = {
							name = mughal_annex_count
							add = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						change_variable = {
							name = mughal_annex_count
							add = 3 # just force it to end
						}
					}
				}
			}
    	}
    }

    option = {
    	name = mughal.7.b

    	ruler = {
    		add_modifier = {
    			name = liberator_of_hindustan
    			months = -1 # permanent bonus
    		}
    	}
    	if = {
    		limit = {
	    		any_scope_character = {
	    			is_heir = yes
	    		}
	    	}
    		heir ?= {
    			add_modifier = {
    				name = scion_of_the_liberator
    				months = -1
    			}
    		}
    	}

    	custom_tooltip = {
    		text = mughal_7_annex_up_to_2_immediate_neighbors

	    	if = {
	    		limit = {
					any_neighbouring_state = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
						owner = {
							is_player = no
							relations:root >= define:NDiplomacy|RELATIONS_THRESHOLD_CORDIAL
							OR = {
								is_subject = no
								is_subject_of = scope:bic_scope
							}
							any_primary_culture = {
								shares_heritage_trait_group_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
	    		}
	    		while = {
	    			limit = {
		    			var:mughal_annex_count < 2
		    		}
					ordered_country = {
						limit = {
							relations:root >= define:NDiplomacy|RELATIONS_THRESHOLD_CORDIAL
							is_player = no
							OR = {
								is_subject = no
								is_subject_of = scope:bic_scope
							}
							any_primary_culture = {
								shares_heritage_trait_group_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT
							}
							any_scope_state = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
								any_neighbouring_state = {
									owner = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						order_by = gdp
						position = 0
						check_range_bounds = no
						save_temporary_scope_as = to_be_annexed
					}

					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:to_be_annexed
							exists = scope:bic_scope
							scope:to_be_annexed = { is_subject_of = scope:bic_scope }
						}
						scope:bic_scope = {
							change_relations = {
								country = root
								value = -10
							}
						}
					}

					if = {
						limit = { exists = scope:to_be_annexed }
						annex = scope:to_be_annexed

						change_variable = {
							name = mughal_annex_count
							add = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						change_variable = {
							name = mughal_annex_count
							add = 3 # just force it to end
						}
					}
				}
			}
    	}
    }

    option = {
    	name = mughal.7.c
    	highlighted_option = yes

    	trigger = {
    		ruler = {
    			NOT = { has_trait = ambitious }
    			age >= define:NCharacters|ADULT_AGE	# only adults can have the ambitious trait
    		}
    	}

    	ruler = {
    		add_trait = ambitious
    		if = {
    			limit = {
    				has_trait = cautious
    			}
    		}
    		random_list = {
    			1 = {
    				# nothing
    			}
    			1 = {
    				remove_trait = cautious
    				add_trait = reckless
    			}
    		}
    	}

    	custom_tooltip = {
    		text = mughal_7_annex_up_to_2_immediate_neighbors

	    	if = {
	    		limit = {
					any_neighbouring_state = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
						owner = {
							is_player = no
							relations:root >= define:NDiplomacy|RELATIONS_THRESHOLD_CORDIAL
							OR = {
								is_subject = no
								is_subject_of = scope:bic_scope
							}
							any_primary_culture = {
								shares_heritage_trait_group_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
	    		}
	    		while = {
	    			limit = {
		    			var:mughal_annex_count < 2
		    		}
					ordered_country = {
						limit = {
							relations:root >= define:NDiplomacy|RELATIONS_THRESHOLD_CORDIAL
							is_player = no
							OR = {
								is_subject = no
								is_subject_of = scope:bic_scope
							}
							any_primary_culture = {
								shares_heritage_trait_group_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT
							}

								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india

						}
						order_by = gdp
						position = 0

					if = {

						scope:bic_scope = {

					}

							name = mughal_annex_count
							add = 3 # just force it to end
						}

    }

    option = {

    	highlighted_option = yes

    		ruler = {
    			age >= define:NCharacters|ADULT_AGE

    				has_trait = meticulous

    			}

    	add_modifier = {

﻿namespace = princely_states


princely_states.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = princely_states.1.t
	desc = princely_states.1.d
	flavor = princely_states.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_protest"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		NOT = { has_variable = princely_states_je_happened }
	}

	immediate = {
        random_overlord_or_above = {
            limit = {
                ROOT = {
                    is_direct_subject_of = prev
                }
            }
            save_scope_as = overlord_scope
        }

	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		is_princely_state = no
	}

	option = {
		name = princely_states.1.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = liberty_desire_boost
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = princely_states.1.b
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = improve_relations_every_princely_state
			every_princely_state = {
				change_relations = {
					country = ROOT
					value = 10
				}
			}
		}

	}
}

					any_neighbouring_state = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
						owner = {
							is_player = no
							relations:root >= define:NDiplomacy|RELATIONS_THRESHOLD_CORDIAL
							is_princely_state = yes
							any_primary_culture = {
								shares_heritage_trait_group_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
	    		}
	    	}
			ordered_country = {
				limit = {
					is_player = no
					relations:root >= define:NDiplomacy|RELATIONS_THRESHOLD_CORDIAL
					is_princely_state = yes
					any_primary_culture = {
						shares_heritage_trait_group_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
						any_neighbouring_state = {
							owner = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				order_by = gdp
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
				save_temporary_scope_as = princely_state_to_be_annexed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:princely_state_to_be_annexed }
				annex = scope:princely_state_to_be_annexed
    		}
	    }
	}
}

# Pack Animal Loan
princely_states.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = princely_states.3.t
	desc = princely_states.3.d
	flavor = princely_states.3.f

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_jungle_expedition"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		is_princely_state = yes
        OR = {
            AND = {
            	AND = { exists = c:GBR is_subject_of = c:GBR }
		        any_country = {
		    		is_afghan_nation = yes
		    		has_war_with = c:GBR
		        }
            }
            AND = {
            	AND = { exists = c:BIC is_subject_of = c:BIC }
				any_country = {
		        	is_afghan_nation = yes
		        	has_war_with = c:BIC
		        }
            }
        }
	}

	immediate = {
		ruler = { save_scope_as = ruler_scope }
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
		        	AND = { exists = c:GBR is_subject_of = c:GBR }
			        any_country = {
			        	is_afghan_nation = yes
			        	has_war_with = c:GBR
			        }
		        }
			}
			c:GBR ?= {
				save_scope_as = top_overlord_scope
			}
		}
		else = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
	            	AND = { exists = c:BIC is_subject_of = c:BIC }
					any_country = {
			        	is_afghan_nation = yes
			        	has_war_with = c:BIC
			        }
	            }
			}
			c:BIC ?= {
				save_scope_as = top_overlord_scope
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
        OR = {
        	is_princely_state = no
        	NOT = {
        		AND = { exists = c:GBR is_subject_of = c:GBR }
        	}
        	NOT = {
        		AND = { exists = c:BIC is_subject_of = c:BIC }
        	}
        }
	}

	option = {
		name = princely_states.3.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:ruler_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = prince_negative_popularity
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = princely_states_camel_stipend
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		post_notification = pack_animal_loan_message
		scope:top_overlord_scope = {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_positive
				target = root
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = princely_states.3.b
		scope:ruler_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = prince_positive_popularity
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = princely_states_ld_action
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

# The Most Exalted Order of the Star of India
princely_states.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = princely_states.4.t
	desc = princely_states.4.d
	flavor = princely_states.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_transfer_of_authority"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = never_fire_again_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		any_scope_character = {
			OR = {
				is_ruler = yes
				has_role = general
			}
		}
		OR = {
            AND = { exists = c:GBR is_subject_of = c:GBR }
            AND = { exists = c:BIC is_subject_of = c:BIC }
        }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_ruler = yes
					has_role = general
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = award_winner_scope
		}
		top_overlord = {
			save_scope_as = top_overlord_scope
		}

	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		is_princely_state = no
	}

	option = {
		name = princely_states.4.a
		default_option = yes
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:top_overlord_scope
			value = 10
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc_feature = lobbies
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
				}
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = { is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord }
				change_appeasement = {
					amount = -1
					factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = princely_states.4.b
		scope:award_winner_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = prince_positive_popularity
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = rejected_overlord_gesture
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:top_overlord_scope
			value = -10
		}
	}
}

princely_states.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = princely_states.5.t
	desc = princely_states.5.d
	flavor = princely_states.5.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_countryside"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		AND = {
			ruler = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = direct
					has_trait = persistent
					has_trait = tactful
					has_trait = ambitious
					has_trait = meticulous
					has_trait = brave
					has_trait = innovative
				}
			}
			c:BIC ?= {
				has_variable = doctrine_of_lapse_cooldown
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = ruler_scope
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = bic_scope
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = top_overlord_scope
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {

	}

	option = {
		name = princely_states.5.a
		default_option = yes
		post_notification = doctrine_of_lapse_reaction_message
		scope:top_overlord_scope = {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = root
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = doctrine_of_lapse_upset
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = princely_states.5.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = overlord_lackey
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc_feature = lobbies
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
				}
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = { is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord }
				change_appeasement = {
					amount = 1
					factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

princely_state_creation.1 = {
	type = country_event

		video = "ep1_transfer_of_authority"
	}

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		c:BIC ?= this
		is_subject = yes
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = organised_baluchistan
		}
		s:STATE_BALUCHISTAN = {
			NOT = {
				any_scope_state = {
					NOR = {
						owner = ROOT
						owner = {
							top_overlord ?= ROOT.top_overlord
						}
						is_treaty_port = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}

			exists = c:KAL
		}

			random_scope_state = {

					}

					}

					create_diplomatic_pact = {
						country = PREV
						type = puppet
					}
				}
				set_variable = princely_state
			}
		}
		create_country = {
			tag = SWT
			origin = this
			province = p:xAFD189
			on_created = {
				set_country_type = unrecognized
				activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
				activate_law = law_type:law_autocracy
				s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN = {
					set_owner_of_provinces = {
						country = c:SWT

					}

						country = PREV

					}
				}
				set_variable = princely_state
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Big Chitral

# Kashmir
princely_state_creation.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:kashmir_state
	title = princely_state_creation.4.t
	desc = princely_state_creation.4.d
	flavor = princely_state_creation.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_transfer_of_authority"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		c:BIC ?= this
		is_subject = yes
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = organised_kashmir
		}
		s:STATE_KASHMIR = {
			NOT = {
				any_scope_state = {
					NOR = {
						owner = ROOT
						owner = {
							top_overlord ?= ROOT.top_overlord
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			exists = c:KAS
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = organised_kashmir
		s:STATE_KASHMIR = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						owner = ROOT
						owner = {
							top_overlord ?= ROOT.top_overlord
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = kashmir_state
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		exists = c:KAS
	}

	option = { # Princely States
		name = princely_state_creation.4.a
		create_country = {
			tag = KAS
			origin = this
			province = p:xF28F90
			on_created = {
				set_country_type = unrecognized
				activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
				activate_law = law_type:law_autocracy
				s:STATE_KASHMIR = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							c:BIC ?= owner
						}
						set_state_owner = c:KAS
					}
				}
				c:BIC ?= {
					create_diplomatic_pact = {
						country = PREV
						type = puppet
					}
				}
				set_variable = princely_state
				set_state_religion = rel:hindu
				ig:ig_devout ?= {
					set_interest_group_name = ig_hindu_priesthood
					remove_ideology = ideology_moralist
					add_ideology = ideology_hindu_moralist
					set_ig_trait = ig_trait:ig_trait_pious_fiction
					set_ig_trait = ig_trait:ig_trait_divine_right
					set_ig_trait = ig_trait:ig_trait_asceticism
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Keep the state
		name = princely_state_creation.4.b
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = upset_every_princely_state_tt
			every_princely_state = {
				change_relations = {
					country = ROOT
					value = -10
				}
				add_liberty_desire = annex_subject_intermediate_desire_change
			}
		}
	}
}

﻿namespace = sepoy_mutiny_events

sepoy_mutiny_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_redcoats"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	title = sepoy_mutiny_events.1.t
	desc = sepoy_mutiny_events.1.d
	flavor = sepoy_mutiny_events.1.f

	duration = 3

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = SAT_nana_saheb_template
			save_scope_as = nana_saheb_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					NOT = {
						root ?= this
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
				}
				save_scope_as = other_revolter_nation_scope
			}
		}
	}

	trigger = {
	}

	option = {
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = SAT_nana_saheb_template
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = SAT_nana_saheb_template
			}
			set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = sepoy_mutiny_peshwa_power
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_reinstated_peshwa
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.1.b
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = SAT_nana_saheb_template
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = sepoy_mutiny_bhat_family_generals
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.1.c
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_turned_away_peshwa
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:other_revolter_nation_scope
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_template = SAT_nana_saheb_template
				}
				transfer_character = scope:other_revolter_nation_scope
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_template = SAT_nana_saheb_template
				}
				kill_character = {
					hidden = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			game_date < 1851.1.28
		}
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.1.e
		create_character = {
			template = SAT_baji_rao_template
		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = SAT_nana_saheb_template
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = sepoy_mutiny_peshwa_power
			days = normal_modifier_time
			multiplier = 1.5
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_reinstated_peshwa
				days = long_modifier_time
				multiplier = 1.5
			}
		}
	}
}

							capital.region = sr:region_punjab

					}
					make_independent = yes
					c:GBR.power_bloc ?= {

					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

						state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = { # Sensible prioritzed snatches
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_MADRAS
						}
						NOT = {
							exists = c:CAR
						}
					}

						tag = CAR
						origin = root
						state = s:STATE_MADRAS.region_state:BIC
						on_created = {
							activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
							activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_MANDALAY
						}
						NOT = {
							exists = c:BUR
						}
					}
					create_country = {
						tag = BUR
						origin = root
						state = s:STATE_MANDALAY.region_state:BIC
						on_created = {
							activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
							activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
							if = {
								limit = {
									c:BIC ?= {
										any_scope_state = {
											state_region = s:STATE_PEGU
										}
									}
								}
								s:STATE_PEGU.region_state:BIC = {
									set_state_owner = c:BUR
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_GUJARAT
						}
						c:BER ?= {
							any_scope_state = {
								state_region = s:STATE_GUJARAT
							}
						}
					}
					s:STATE_GUJARAT.region_state:BIC = {
						set_state_owner = c:BER
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_TENASSERIM
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							c:BUR ?= {
								any_scope_state = {
									state_region = s:STATE_PEGU
								}
							}
						}
						s:STATE_TENASSERIM.region_state:BIC = {
							set_state_owner = c:BUR
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							c:SIA ?= {
								any_scope_state = {

								}
							}
						}

							OR = {
								capital.region = sr:region_bombay
								capital.region = sr:region_bengal
								capital.region = sr:region_punjab
								capital.region = sr:region_central_india
								capital.state_region = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
								capital.state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					any_primary_culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
					}
					OR = {
						capital.region = sr:region_bombay
						capital.region = sr:region_bengal
						capital.region = sr:region_punjab
						capital.region = sr:region_central_india
						capital.state_region = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
						capital.state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = prince_scope
				root = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								region = sr:region_punjab
								region = sr:region_central_india
								region = sr:region_bombay
								region = sr:region_bengal
								region = sr:region_himalayas
								state_region = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
								state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
							}
							any_neighbouring_state = {
								scope:prince_scope ?= owner
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = scope:prince_scope
					}
				}
			}
			clear_saved_scope = prince_scope
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_madras
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = rel:hindu
				value = large_radicals
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = rel:sunni
				value = large_radicals
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.2.e
		highlighted_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
		}
		trigger = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_subject_or_below ?= {
					OR = {
						capital.region = sr:region_bengal
						capital.region = sr:region_madras
						capital.region = sr:region_punjab
						capital.region = sr:region_central_india
						capital.state_region = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
						capital.state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = india_retreat_to_bombay_tt
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_subject_of = root
						OR = {
							capital.region = sr:region_bengal
							capital.region = sr:region_madras
							capital.region = sr:region_punjab
							capital.region = sr:region_central_india
							capital.state_region = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
							capital.state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
						}
					}
					make_independent = yes
					c:GBR.power_bloc ?= {
						add_leverage = { target = PREV value = -500 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_punjab
						region = sr:region_central_india
						region = sr:region_bombay
						region = sr:region_bengal
						region = sr:region_himalayas
						state_region = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
						state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = { # Sensible prioritzed snatches
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_MADRAS
						}
						NOT = {
							exists = c:CAR
						}
					}
					create_country = {
						tag = CAR
						origin = root
						state = s:STATE_MADRAS.region_state:BIC
						on_created = {
							activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
							activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy

					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_MANDALAY
						}
						NOT = {
							exists = c:BUR
						}
					}
					create_country = {
						tag = BUR
						origin = root
						state = s:STATE_MANDALAY.region_state:BIC
						on_created = {
							activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
							activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
							if = {
								limit = {
									c:BIC ?= {
										any_scope_state = {
											state_region = s:STATE_PEGU
										}
									}
								}
								s:STATE_PEGU.region_state:BIC = {
									set_state_owner = c:BUR
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_TENASSERIM
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							c:BUR ?= {
								any_scope_state = {
									state_region = s:STATE_PEGU
								}
							}
						}
						s:STATE_TENASSERIM.region_state:BIC = {
							set_state_owner = c:BUR
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							c:SIA ?= {
								any_scope_state = {
									state_region = s:STATE_TENASSERIM
								}
							}
						}
						s:STATE_TENASSERIM.region_state:BIC = {
							set_state_owner = c:SIA
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								exists = c:KRN
							}
						}
						create_country = {
							tag = KRN
							origin = root
							state = s:STATE_TENASSERIM.region_state:BIC
							on_created = {
								activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
								activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_TRAVANCORE
						}
						OR = {
							c:COC ?= {
								any_scope_state = {
									state_region = s:STATE_TRAVANCORE
								}
							}
							c:TRA ?= {
								any_scope_state = {
									state_region = s:STATE_TRAVANCORE
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							c:COC ?= {
								any_scope_state = {
									state_region = s:STATE_TRAVANCORE
								}
							}
						}
						s:STATE_TRAVANCORE.region_state:BIC = {
							set_state_owner = c:COC
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							c:TRA ?= {
								any_scope_state = {
									state_region = s:STATE_TRAVANCORE
								}
							}
						}
						s:STATE_TRAVANCORE.region_state:BIC = {
							set_state_owner = c:TRA
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_CIRCARS
						}
						c:HYD ?= {
							owns_entire_state_region = STATE_HYDERABAD
						}
					}
					s:STATE_CIRCARS.region_state:BIC = {
						set_state_owner = c:HYD
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_KURNOOL
						}
						c:HYD ?= {
							owns_entire_state_region = STATE_HYDERABAD
						}
					}
					s:STATE_KURNOOL.region_state:BIC = {
						set_state_owner = c:HYD
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_DELHI
						}
						c:MUG ?= {
							any_scope_state = {
								state_region = s:STATE_DELHI
							}
						}
					}
					s:STATE_DELHI.region_state:BIC = {
						set_state_owner = c:MUG
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_DELHI
						}
						NOT = {
							exists = c:MUG
						}
					}
					create_country = {
						tag = MUG
						origin = root
						state = s:STATE_DELHI.region_state:BIC
						on_created = {
							activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
							activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_AGRA
						}
						exists = c:MUG
					}
					s:STATE_AGRA.region_state:BIC = {
						set_state_owner = c:MUG
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_AWADH
						}
						NOT = {
							exists = c:AWA
						}
					}
					create_country = {
						tag = AWA
						origin = root
						state = s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC
						on_created = {
							activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
							activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_AWADH
						}
						exists = c:MUG
					}
					s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC = {
						set_state_owner = c:MUG
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_CENTRAL_PROVINCES
						}
						c:NAG ?= {
							any_scope_state = {
								state_region = s:STATE_CENTRAL_PROVINCES
							}
						}
					}
					s:STATE_CENTRAL_PROVINCES.region_state:BIC = {
						set_state_owner = c:NAG
					}
				}
			}
		}
		while = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_punjab
						region = sr:region_central_india
						region = sr:region_madras
						region = sr:region_bengal
						region = sr:region_himalayas
						state_region = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
						state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
					}
					any_neighbouring_state = {
						owner = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
							}
							OR = {
								capital.region = sr:region_madras
								capital.region = sr:region_bengal
								capital.region = sr:region_punjab
								capital.region = sr:region_central_india
								capital.state_region = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
								capital.state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					any_primary_culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
					}
					OR = {
						capital.region = sr:region_madras
						capital.region = sr:region_bengal
						capital.region = sr:region_punjab
						capital.region = sr:region_central_india
						capital.state_region = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
						capital.state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = prince_scope
				root = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								region = sr:region_punjab
								region = sr:region_central_india
								region = sr:region_madras
								region = sr:region_bengal
								region = sr:region_himalayas
								state_region = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
								state_region = s:STATE_QUETTA
							}
							any_neighbouring_state = {
								scope:prince_scope ?= owner
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = scope:prince_scope
					}
				}
			}
			clear_saved_scope = prince_scope
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {

sepoy_mutiny_events.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = sepoy_mutiny_events.4.t
	desc = sepoy_mutiny_events.4.d
	flavor = sepoy_mutiny_events.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "asia_sepoy_mutiny"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:SAT
			}
			c:SAT ?= {
				save_scope_as = satara_scope
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:MUG
			}
			c:MUG ?= {
				save_scope_as = mughals_scope
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:BIC
			}
			c:BIC ?= {
				save_scope_as = eic_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.4.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					gdp >= 2000000
				}
				add = 40
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					any_scope_play_involved = { c:SAT ?= THIS }
					any_scope_play_involved = { c:BIC ?= THIS }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				}
				OR = {
					capital = {
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_bombay
							region = sr:region_madras
						}
					}
					root = {
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:marathi
					}
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_play_involved = { c:SAT ?= THIS }
					any_scope_play_involved = { c:BIC ?= THIS }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				}
				add_target_backers = { root }
				add_war_goal = { holder = c:BIC target_country = root type = annex_country } # Historically, all princely states that rebelled were annexed.
				sepoy_mutiny_tag_specific_war_goals = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					any_scope_play_involved = { c:MUG ?= THIS }
					any_scope_play_involved = { c:BIC ?= THIS }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				}
				capital = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_bengal
						region = sr:region_punjab
						region = sr:region_central_india
						region = sr:region_himalayas
						region = sr:region_persia
					}
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_play_involved = { c:MUG ?= THIS }
					any_scope_play_involved = { c:BIC ?= THIS }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				}
				add_target_backers = { root }
				add_war_goal = { holder = c:BIC target_country = root type = annex_country } # Historically, all princely states that rebelled were annexed.
				sepoy_mutiny_tag_specific_war_goals = yes
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = war_for_independence
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.4.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					capital = {
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_bengal
							region = sr:region_punjab
							region = sr:region_central_india
							region = sr:region_himalayas
							region = sr:region_persia
						}
					}
				}
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						religion = rel:sunni
						religion = rel:shiite
					}
				}
				add = 20
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			any_diplomatic_play = {
				any_scope_play_involved = { c:MUG ?= THIS }
				any_scope_play_involved = { c:BIC ?= THIS }
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:MUG ?= {
					is_country_type = unrecognized
				}
			}
			c:MUG ?= {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = root
					type = vassal
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			c:MUG ?= {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = root
					type = puppet
				}
			}
		}
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_play_involved = { c:MUG ?= THIS }
				any_scope_play_involved = { c:BIC ?= THIS }
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			}
			add_target_backers = { root }
			add_war_goal = { holder = c:BIC target_country = root type = annex_country } # Historically, all princely states that rebelled were annexed.
			sepoy_mutiny_tag_specific_war_goals = yes
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = war_for_independence
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.4.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						capital = {
							OR = {
								region = sr:region_bombay
								region = sr:region_madras
							}
						}
					}
				}
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:marathi
				}
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					religion = rel:hindu
				}
				add = 20
			}
		}

		trigger = {
			any_diplomatic_play = {
				any_scope_play_involved = { c:SAT ?= THIS }
				any_scope_play_involved = { c:BIC ?= THIS }
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:SAT ?= {
					is_country_type = unrecognized
				}
			}
			c:SAT ?= {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = root
					type = vassal
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			c:SAT ?= {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = root
					type = puppet
				}
			}
		}
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_play_involved = { c:SAT ?= THIS }
				any_scope_play_involved = { c:BIC ?= THIS }
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			}
			add_target_backers = { root }
			add_war_goal = { holder = c:BIC target_country = root type = annex_country } # Historically, all princely states that rebelled were annexed.
			sepoy_mutiny_tag_specific_war_goals = yes
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = war_for_independence
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.4.e
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			create_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = puppet
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = large_radicals
		}
	}
}

	}

	option = {
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.11.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = home_rule_unavailable_tt
		custom_tooltip = end_company_rule_made_available_tt
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = upgrade_company_rule_modifier_tt
			remove_modifier = modifier_india_company_rule
			add_modifier = modifier_india_company_rule_upgraded
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sepoy_mutiny_events.11.b
		custom_tooltip = home_rule_unavailable_tt
		custom_tooltip = end_company_rule_made_available_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_technology_researched = central_archives
				}
			}
			add_technology_researched = central_archives
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law = law_type:law_secret_police
				}
			}
			activate_law = law_type:law_secret_police
		}
		add_modifier = modifier_indian_imperial_police
	}
}
﻿namespace = set_hierarchy_event


set_hierarchy_event.1 = {
	type = country_event
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		has_social_hierarchy = default_social_hierarchy
		AND = {
			AND = { exists = c:GBR is_subject_of = c:GBR }
			religion_percent_country = {
				target = rel:hindu
				value >= 0.50
			}
			owner = {
				any_primary_culture = {
					has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = { # Replaces the default hierarchy and sets the Caste Not Enforced law
		owner = {
			save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope
		}
		set_social_hierarchy = british_indian_caste_system
		activate_law = law_type:law_hindu_caste_not_enforced
		post_notification = caste_hierarchy_appears
	}

}


set_hierarchy_event.2 = {
	type = country_event
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		has_social_hierarchy = british_indian_caste_system
		religion_percent_country = {
			target = rel:hindu
			value <= 0.10
		}
	}

	immediate = { # Replaces the British Indian Caste System
		owner = {
			save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope
		}
		set_social_hierarchy = default_social_hierarchy
		post_notification = caste_hierarchy_disappears
	}

}

# For c:PAN, The Sikh Empire

# Ranjit Singh Hosts a Wedding
sikh_empire.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sikh_empire.1.t
	desc = sikh_empire.1.d
	flavor = sikh_empire.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_cross_cultural_festival"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_sikh_sovereignty
	}

	immediate = {
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = bic_scope
		}
		c:PAN ?= {
			save_scope_as = pan_scope
		}

	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		ruler = { NOT = { has_template = pan_ranjit_singh } }
	}

	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.1.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GBR
			value = 5
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			post_notification = sikh_wedding_diplomacy_message
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_positive
				target = root
			}
			change_relations = {
				country = c:PAN
				value = 5
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = sikh_empire.2 days = 30 popup = yes }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = wedding_prestige
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = sikh_empire.2 days = 30 popup = yes }
		}
	}
}

# Neighbour attacks PAN after royal wedding
sikh_empire.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sikh_empire.2.t
	desc = sikh_empire.2.d
	flavor = sikh_empire.2.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_ca_mountain_pass_military"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_sikh_sovereignty
		any_scope_state = {
			state_region = S:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
		}
		any_neighbouring_state = {
			owner = {
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = S:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}
		random_neighbouring_state = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
				}
			}
			owner = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_country
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			ruler = { NOT = { has_template = pan_ranjit_singh } }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_country }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.2.a
		default_option = yes
		c:PAN ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = scope:relevant_country
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = sikh_conscription
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.2.b
		c:PAN ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = scope:relevant_country
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = show_of_strength
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Death of the Sher-e-Punjab (Maharaja Ranjit Singh's Death)
sikh_empire.3 = {
	type = country_event
	title = sikh_empire.3.t
	desc = sikh_empire.3.d
	flavor = sikh_empire.3.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	left_icon = scope:relevant_ruler

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_sikh_sovereignty
		c:PAN ?= this
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = pan_ranjit_singh
		}
		any_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
			owner = {
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = pan_ranjit_singh
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ruler
		}
		random_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_country
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = bic_scope
		}
		save_scope_as = pan_scope
	}
	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.3.a
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = ranjit_singh_death_tt
			heir ?= {
				set_character_immortal = no
			}
			scope:relevant_ruler = {
				kill_character = yes # Ranjit Singh dies, Kharak Singh will become ruler
			}
			create_character = {
				# Nau Nihal Singh is the new heir
				template = pan_nau_nihal_singh
			}
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			post_notification = eic_encroaching_pan_message
			change_relations = {
				country = c:PAN
				value = -10
			}
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = root
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = intrigue_reduced_by_20_tt
			je:je_sikh_sovereignty ?= {
				add_progress = { value = -20 name = sikh_empire_intrigue_bar }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Threat of Rebellion
sikh_empire.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sikh_empire.4.t
	desc = sikh_empire.4.d
	flavor = sikh_empire.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_courtroom_upheaval"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_sikh_sovereignty
		NOT = {
			ruler = {
				has_template = pan_ranjit_singh
			}
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_role = general
			is_busy = no
			is_ruler = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				is_busy = no
				is_ruler = no
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_general
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.4.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_general = {
			add_character_role = agitator
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = intrigue_increased_by_10_tt
			je:je_sikh_sovereignty ?= {
				add_progress = { value = 10 name = sikh_empire_intrigue_bar }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.4.b
		trigger = {
			scope:relevant_general = {
				NOT = {
					this.interest_group ?= {
						is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
					}
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = intrigue_reduced_by_10_tt
			je:je_sikh_sovereignty ?= {
				add_progress = { value = -10 name = sikh_empire_intrigue_bar }
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			interest_group = ruler.interest_group
		}
	}
}

# A Grave Injury (Pillar Falls on Nau Nihal Singh)
sikh_empire.6 = {
	type = country_event
	title = sikh_empire.6.t
	desc = sikh_empire.6.d
	flavor = sikh_empire.6.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	left_icon = scope:relevant_ruler

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_sikh_sovereignty
	}

	immediate = {
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = relevant_ruler
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_template = pan_dhian_singh
				}
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_template = pan_dhian_singh
				}
				save_scope_as = dhian_singh_scope
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_neighbouring_state = {
					owner = {
						AND = {
							NOT = {
								c:PAN ?= this
							}
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_neighbouring_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = {
						AND = {
							NOT = {
								c:PAN ?= this
							}
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
						}
					}
				}
				owner = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_country
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.6.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = nau_nihal_dies_tt
			scope:relevant_ruler = {
				kill_character = yes # Nau Nihal Singh dies, Sher Singh will become ruler
			}
			create_character = {
				# Duleep Singh is the new heir
				# Duleep is the last heir from a character template we spawn in this chain of events for the Sikh Sovereignty JE
				template = pan_duleep_singh
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_template = pan_dhian_singh
				}
			}
			scope:dhian_singh_scope = {
				remove_trait = experienced_political_operator
				add_trait = expert_political_operator
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:relevant_country
			}
			post_notification = neighbor_encroaching_pan_message
			change_relations = {
				country = c:BIC
				value = -5
			}
			scope:relevant_country ?= {
				create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
					type = catalyst_event_negative
					target = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		je:je_sikh_sovereignty ?= {
			add_progress = { value = -50 name = sikh_empire_intrigue_bar }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.6.b
		trigger = { # Player can choose to try to save Nau Nihal Singh against the Wazir's advice. If Nau Nihal Singh survives he is very injured.
			is_player = yes
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = nau_nihal_dies_tt
					scope:relevant_ruler = {
						kill_character = yes # Nau Nihal Singh dies, Sher Singh will become ruler
					}
					create_character = {
						# Duleep Singh is the new heir
						# Duleep is the last heir from a character template we spawn in this chain of events for the Sikh Sovereignty JE
						template = pan_duleep_singh
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:relevant_country
					}
					post_notification = neighbor_encroaching_pan_message
					scope:relevant_country ?= {
						create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
							type = catalyst_event_negative
							target = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				je:je_sikh_sovereignty ?= {
					add_progress = { value = -50 name = sikh_empire_intrigue_bar }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = nau_nihal_lives_tt
				}
				scope:relevant_ruler = {
					add_trait = wounded
					add_trait = psychological_affliction
					add_trait = scarred
					remove_trait = expensive_tastes
					remove_trait = pious
					add_modifier = {
						name = nau_nihal_survives # Character health impacted for life, he was definitely near death
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:relevant_country
					}
					post_notification = neighbor_encroaching_pan_message
					change_relations = {
						country = scope:relevant_country
						value = -5
					}
					scope:relevant_country ?= {
						create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
							type = catalyst_event_negative
							target = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = intrigue_reduced_by_20_tt
					je:je_sikh_sovereignty ?= {
						add_progress = { value = -20 name = sikh_empire_intrigue_bar }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# End of a Dynasty (je_sikh_sovereignty, on fail)
sikh_empire.7 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = sikh_empire.7.t
				trigger = {
					is_subject = yes
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = sikh_empire.7.t2 # Loc if JE failed with 5 Majaraja deaths
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = sikh_empire.7.d
				trigger = {
					is_subject = yes
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = sikh_empire.7.d2 # Loc if JE failed with 5 Majaraja deaths
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = sikh_empire.7.f # Loc if GBR becomes overlord
				trigger = {
					AND = {
						is_subject = yes
						top_overlord = {
							c:GBR ?= this
						}
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = sikh_empire.7.f2 # Loc if JE failed with 5 Majaraja deaths or has a top overlord that is not GBR
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_transfer_of_authority"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_sikh_sovereignty
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_template = pan_duleep_singh
				}
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_template = pan_duleep_singh
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_character
		}
		else = {
			ruler = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_character
			}
		}

	}

	cancellation_trigger = {

	}

	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.7.a
		default_option = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = sikh_empire_je_done
		}
		if = { # For exiling Duleep Singh if he is very young
			limit = {
				ruler = {
					age < 18
					has_template = pan_duleep_singh
				}
			}
			ruler = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = duleep_goes_to_gbr_tt
					place_character_in_void = 1200
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				ruler = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = { # For sending Duleep Singh to GBR if he happens to be at least 18
			limit = {
				ruler = {
					age >= 18
					has_template = pan_duleep_singh
				}
				top_overlord = {
					c:GBR ?= this
				}
			}
			ruler = {
				add_character_role = agitator
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = duleep_goes_to_gbr_tt
					transfer_character = c:GBR
				}
				change_character_religion = rel:protestant
			}
		} # For exiling any other ruler if Sikh Empire becomes a subject
		else = {
			ruler = {
				exile_character = yes
			}
			create_character = {
				ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

#  Legacy of the Lion (je_sikh_sovereignty, on complete)
sikh_empire.8 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sikh_empire.8.t
	desc = sikh_empire.8.d
	flavor = sikh_empire.8.f

	event_image = {
		video = "asia_hinduism_sikhism"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_sikh_sovereignty
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = pan_duleep_singh
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = duleep_character
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {

	}

	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.8.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = sikh_sovereignty_success
			days = very_long_modifier_time
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		ruler = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = lions_legacy
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = sikh_empire_je_done
		}
	}
}

# The Last Maharaja (Freeing Duleep Singh from the void)
sikh_empire.9 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sikh_empire.9.t
	desc = sikh_empire.9.d
	flavor = sikh_empire.9.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	left_icon = scope:duleep_character

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_sikh_sovereignty
		c:GBR ?= this
		has_dlc_feature = ip2_content
		game_date >= 1856.9.6 # After Duleep is 18
		any_character_in_void = {
			has_template = pan_duleep_singh
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_template = pan_duleep_singh
			}
			save_scope_as = duleep_character
		}

	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = c:PAN }
			NOT = { exists = c:GBR }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.9.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:duleep_character = {
			hidden_effect = {
				free_character_from_void = yes
			}
			add_trait = expensive_tastes
			add_character_role = agitator
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = duleep_goes_to_gbr_tt
				transfer_character = c:GBR
			}
			change_character_religion = rel:protestant
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.9.b
		scope:duleep_character = {
			hidden_effect = {
				free_character_from_void = yes
			}
			exile_character = yes
			add_character_role = agitator
			add_trait = hedonist
		}
	}
}

# Sher Singh gets blasted with a shotgun
sikh_empire.10 = {
	type = country_event
	title = sikh_empire.10.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = sikh_empire.10.d1
				trigger = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_role = politician
						has_trait = wrathful
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = sikh_empire.10.d2
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = sikh_empire.10.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	left_icon = scope:relevant_ruler

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_sikh_sovereignty
		c:PAN ?= this
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = pan_sher_singh
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = pan_sher_singh
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ruler
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = politician
				has_trait = wrathful
			}
			save_scope_as = assassin_scope
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = bic_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.10.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = sher_singh_death_tt
			scope:relevant_ruler = {
				kill_character = { # Sher Singh dies, Duleep Singh will become ruler
					hidden = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = pan_duleep_singh
				}
			}
			heir ?= {
				disinherit_character = yes
			}
		}
		c:PAN ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = scope:bic_scope
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = intrigue_reduced_by_50_tt
			je:je_sikh_sovereignty ?= {
				add_progress = { value = -50 name = sikh_empire_intrigue_bar }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Generic assassination
sikh_empire.11 = {
	type = country_event
	title = sikh_empire.11.t
	desc = sikh_empire.11.d
	flavor = sikh_empire.11.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	left_icon = scope:relevant_ruler

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_sikh_sovereignty
		c:PAN ?= this
	}

	cooldown = { days = 100 }

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				is_ruler = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ruler
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = bic_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sikh_empire.11.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				ruler = { has_template = pan_kharak_singh }
			}
			scope:relevant_ruler = {
				kill_character = yes # Kharak Singh dies, Nau Nihal Singh will become ruler
			}
			create_character = {
				# Sher Singh is the new heir
				template = pan_sher_singh
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ruler = { has_template = pan_nau_nihal_singh }
			}
			scope:relevant_ruler = {
				kill_character = yes # Nau Nihal Singh dies, Sher Singh will become ruler
			}
			create_character = {
				# Duleep Singh is the new heir
				# Duleep is the last heir from a character template we spawn in this chain of events for the Sikh Sovereignty JE
				template = pan_duleep_singh
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ruler = { has_template = pan_sher_singh }
			}
			scope:relevant_ruler = {
				kill_character = yes # Sher Singh dies, Duleep Singh will become ruler
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					heir ?= {
						has_template = pan_duleep_singh
					}
				}
				heir ?= {
					disinherit_character = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:relevant_ruler = {
				kill_character = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						OR = {
							has_template = pan_sher_singh
							has_template = pan_duleep_singh
							has_template = pan_kharak_singh
							has_template = pan_nau_nihal_singh
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_role = general
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_role = general
					}
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				ordered_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_role = politician
					}
					order_by = interest_group.ig_clout
					position = 0
					check_range_bounds = no
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = intrigue_reduced_by_50_tt
			je:je_sikh_sovereignty ?= {
				add_progress = { value = -50 name = sikh_empire_intrigue_bar }
			}
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = utilitarian

# Good Governance For All, on JE completion
utilitarian.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = utilitarian.1.t
	desc = utilitarian.1.d
	flavor = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = utilitarian.1.f # Loc if a Utilitarian character exists
				trigger = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_utilitarian_leader
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = utilitarian.1.f2 # Loc if no Utilitarian character exists
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_india_factory_floor"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by Imperialism of Promise JE
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_utilitarian_leader
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_utilitarian_leader
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_character
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = c:BIC
			}
			NOT = {
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:british
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = utilitarian.1.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = utilitarian_bonus
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_malthusian
				}
				remove_ideology = ideology_malthusian
				add_ideology = ideology_utilitarian_millian
			}
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					OR = {
						has_role = agitator
						has_role = politician
					}
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_utilitarian_leader
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = util_colonial_admin_success
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# A Project Without Progress, on JE time out
utilitarian.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = utilitarian.3.t
	desc = utilitarian.3.d
	flavor = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = utilitarian.3.f # Loc if a Utilitarian character exists
				trigger = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_utilitarian_leader
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = utilitarian.3.f2 # Loc if no Utilitarian character exists
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	event_image = {
		video = "asia_politician_parliament_motion"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by Imperialism of Promise JE
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_utilitarian_leader
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_utilitarian_leader
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_character
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = util_end_var
			value = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				root.average_sol >= 10
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = util_end_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = util_end_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_compulsory_primary_school
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = util_end_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_public_schools
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = util_end_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_no_womens_rights
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = util_end_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = c:BIC
			}
			NOT = {
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:british
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = utilitarian.3.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = utilitarian_partial_malus
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:util_end_var >= 1
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						OR = {
							has_role = agitator
							has_role = politician
						}
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_utilitarian_leader
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = util_colonial_admin_partial_success
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:util_end_var > 2
			}
			ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_malthusian
					}
					remove_ideology = ideology_malthusian
					add_ideology = ideology_utilitarian_millian
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Danger to Imperial Mission
utilitarian.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:relevant_state
	title = utilitarian.5.t
	desc = utilitarian.5.d
	flavor = utilitarian.5.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ip2_intercommunal_tensions"
	}

	dlc = dlc011

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_scope_state = {
			has_variable = utilitarian_effort_underway
			any_scope_pop = {
				state = {
					state_cultural_acceptance = {
						target = PREV.culture
						value < acceptance_status_4
					}
				}
			}
			any_scope_pop = {
				culture = {
					has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone
				}
			}
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			OR = {
				has_role = agitator
				has_role = politician
			}
			has_ideology = ideology:ideology_utilitarian_leader
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					state = {
						state_cultural_acceptance = {
							target = PREV.culture
							value < acceptance_status_4
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = discriminated_culture_pop
				culture = { save_scope_as = discriminated_culture }
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = primary_culture_pop
				culture = { save_scope_as = not_discriminated_culture }
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_role = agitator
					has_role = politician
				}
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_utilitarian_leader
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_character
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = c:BIC
			}
			NOT = {
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:british
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # "favored" option for Utilitarian ideology
		name = utilitarian.5.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				culture = scope:discriminated_culture
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = restrained_fortune_seekers
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = utilitarian.5.b
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				culture = scope:discriminated_culture
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = unrestrained_fortune_seekers
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}
